   297	async def _load_supply_orders_read(
   298	    session: AsyncSession,
   299	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   300	    supply_id: uuid.UUID,
   301	) -> list[FbsOrder]:
   302	    result = await session.execute(_supply_orders_stmt(tenant_id, supply_id))
   303	    return list(result.scalars().all())

   306	async def _get_supply_read(
   307	    session: AsyncSession,
   308	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   309	    supply_id: uuid.UUID,
   310	    *,
   311	    with_trbxes: bool = False,
   312	) -> FbsSupply | None:
   313	    stmt = select(FbsSupply).where(
   314	        FbsSupply.id == supply_id,
   315	        FbsSupply.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   316	    )
   317	    if with_trbxes:
   318	        stmt = stmt.options(selectinload(FbsSupply.trbxes))
   319	    result = await session.execute(stmt)
   320	    return result.scalar_one_or_none()

   323	def _order_has_sgtin_marking(order: FbsOrder) -> bool:
   324	    return current_order_marking(list(order.markings), MARKING_KIND_SGTIN) is not None

   326	
   327	async def _sync_supply_orders_from_wb(
   328	    session: AsyncSession,
   329	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   330	    supply: FbsSupply,
   331	    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
   332	    token: str,
   333	    *,
   334	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   335	) -> list[FbsOrder]:
   336	    orders = await _load_supply_orders_read(session, tenant_id, supply.id)
   337	    if not orders:
   338	        supply.last_wb_sync_at = datetime.now(UTC)
   339	        await session.flush()
   340	        return orders
   341	
   342	    wb_ids = [int(order.wb_order_id) for order in orders]
   343	    for batch in split_marketplace_order_id_batches(wb_ids):
   344	        try:
   345	            status_rows = await fetch_marketplace_orders_status(
   346	                http_client,
   347	                api_token=token,
   348	                order_ids=batch,
   349	            )
   350	        except WildberriesClientError as exc:
   351	            raise _shipment_error_from_wb(
   352	                exc,
   353	                tenant_id=tenant_id,
   354	                seller_id=supply.seller_id,
   355	                local_entity_id=supply.id,
   356	                wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
   357	                event="fbs shipment WB supply status sync failed",
   358	                extra_context={"wb_order_ids": batch},
   359	            ) from exc
   360	        by_id = {int(row["id"]): row for row in status_rows if row.get("id") is not None}
   361	        for order in orders:
   362	            row = by_id.get(int(order.wb_order_id))
   363	            if row is None:
   364	                continue
   365	            wb_status = _wb_status_from_row(row)
   366	            supplier_status = _supplier_status_from_row(row)
   367	            if wb_status is not None or supplier_status is not None:
   368	                await _apply_wb_status_to_order(
   369	                    session,
   370	                    order,
   371	                    wb_status,
   372	                    supplier_status=supplier_status,
   373	                    actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   374	                )
   375	
   376	    for order in orders:
   377	        with suppress(marking_svc.FbsMarkingError):
   378	            await marking_svc.sync_order_marking_statuses(
   379	                session,
   380	                tenant_id,
   381	                order.id,
   382	                http_client,
   383	                actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   384	            )
   385	
   386	    supply.last_wb_sync_at = datetime.now(UTC)
   387	    await session.flush()
   388	    await session.refresh(supply)
   389	    return await _load_supply_orders_read(session, tenant_id, supply.id)
   390	
   391	
   392	def _compute_preflight_version(
   393	    supply: FbsSupply,
   394	    orders: list[FbsOrder],
   395	    *,
   396	    cargo_qr_ready: bool,
   397	    has_physical_boxes: bool,
   398	    without_distribution: bool,
   399	    unassigned_packed_order_ids: frozenset[uuid.UUID],
   400	) -> str:
   401	    parts = [
   402	        str(supply.id),
   403	        supply.status,
   404	        supply.delivery_type,
   405	        str(cargo_qr_ready),
   406	        str(has_physical_boxes),
   407	        str(without_distribution),
   408	        *(str(order_id) for order_id in sorted(unassigned_packed_order_ids)),
   409	    ]
   410	    for order in sorted(orders, key=lambda item: item.id):
   411	        parts.extend(
   412	            [
   413	                str(order.id),
   414	                order.status,
   415	                order.wb_status or "",
   416	                str(order.metadata_delivery_allowed),
   417	            ]
   418	        )
   419	    raw = "|".join(parts).encode()
   420	    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
   421	
   422	
   423	def _build_delivery_checks(
   424	    supply: FbsSupply,
   425	    orders: list[FbsOrder],
   426	    *,
   427	    cargo_qr_ready: bool,
   428	    boxes_required: bool = True,
   429	    has_physical_boxes: bool = True,
   430	    without_distribution: bool = False,
   431	    unassigned_packed_order_ids: frozenset[uuid.UUID] = frozenset(),
   432	) -> list[DeliveryCheck]:
   433	    # «Сдать без Честного знака» снимает НАШЕ требование маркировки по поставке.
   434	    # Требование самого Wildberries, записанное в required_meta_json заказа, этим
   435	    # флагом не отменяется: такой заказ по-прежнему не уедет, и это правильно.
   436	    honest_sign_skipped = supply.honest_sign_skipped_at is not None
   437	    checks: list[DeliveryCheck] = []
   438	
   439	    if supply.status in _DELIVER_BLOCKED_SUPPLY_STATUSES:
   440	        checks.append(
   441	            DeliveryCheck(
   442	                code="supply_bad_status",
   443	                message="Поставка уже передана или закрыта.",
   444	                ok=False,
   445	            )
   446	        )
   447	    elif supply.status != FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_PACKED:
   448	        checks.append(
   449	            DeliveryCheck(
   450	                code="packaging_required",
   451	                message="Упаковка поставки не завершена.",
   452	                ok=False,
   453	            )
   454	        )
   455	    else:
   456	        checks.append(
   457	            DeliveryCheck(
   458	                code="supply_packed",
   459	                message="Поставка упакована.",
   460	                ok=True,

    30	class FbsPackagingFulfillment(Base):
    31	    """Links one physical packaging unit to exactly one FBS order."""
    32	
    33	    __tablename__ = "fbs_packaging_fulfillments"
    34	    __table_args__ = (
    35	        UniqueConstraint(
    36	            "packaging_task_id",
    37	            "pack_idempotency_key",
    38	            name="uq_fbs_packaging_fulfillments_task_idempotency",
    39	        ),
    40	        Index(
    41	            "uq_fbs_packaging_fulfillments_active_order",
    42	            "fbs_order_id",
    43	            unique=True,
    44	            postgresql_where=text("undone_at IS NULL"),
    45	        ),
    46	    )
    47	
    48	    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    49	    tenant_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
    50	        Uuid(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("tenants.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True
    51	    )
    52	    fbs_order_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
    53	        Uuid(as_uuid=True),
    54	        ForeignKey("fbs_orders.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
    55	        nullable=False,
    56	        index=True,
    57	    )
    58	    packaging_task_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
    59	        Uuid(as_uuid=True),
    60	        ForeignKey("packaging_tasks.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
    61	        nullable=False,
    62	        index=True,
    63	    )
    64	    packaging_task_line_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
    65	        Uuid(as_uuid=True),
    66	        ForeignKey("packaging_task_lines.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
    67	        nullable=False,
    68	        index=True,
    69	    )
    70	    fulfilled_by_user_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(
    71	        Uuid(as_uuid=True),
    72	        ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"),
    73	        nullable=True,
    74	    )
    75	    fulfilled_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    76	    inventory_movement_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(
    77	        Uuid(as_uuid=True),
    78	        ForeignKey("inventory_movements.id", ondelete="SET NULL"),
    79	        nullable=True,
    80	    )
    81	    pack_idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    82	    ozon_packed_units_json: Mapped[list[dict[str, str]] | None] = mapped_column(JSON, nullable=True)
    83	    undone_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    84	    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
    85	        DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), nullable=False
    86	    )
    87	
    88	    tenant: Mapped[Tenant] = relationship("Tenant")
    89	    order: Mapped[FbsOrder] = relationship("FbsOrder", back_populates="packaging_fulfillments")
    90	    packaging_task: Mapped[PackagingTask] = relationship(

   700	                unpacked >= quantity,
   701	            )
   702	            .values(
   703	                quantity_unpacked=unpacked - quantity,
   704	                quantity_packed=packed + quantity,
   705	                quantity=unpacked + packed,
   706	                updated_at=datetime.now(UTC),
   707	            )
   708	        )
   709	    else:
   710	        moved_from_unpacked = case((unpacked >= quantity, quantity), else_=unpacked)
   711	        stmt = (
   712	            update(InventoryBalance)
   713	            .where(
   714	                InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   715	                InventoryBalance.product_id == product_id,
   716	                InventoryBalance.storage_location_id == storage_location_id,
   717	                InventoryBalance.container_kind.is_(None),
   718	                InventoryBalance.container_id.is_(None),
   719	                unpacked + packed >= quantity,
   720	            )
   721	            .values(
   722	                quantity_unpacked=unpacked - moved_from_unpacked,
   723	                quantity_packed=packed + moved_from_unpacked,
   724	                quantity=unpacked + packed,
   725	                updated_at=datetime.now(UTC),
   726	            )
   727	        )
   728	    updated_balance_id = await session.scalar(
   729	        stmt.returning(InventoryBalance.id).execution_options(synchronize_session=False)
   730	    )
   731	    if updated_balance_id is None:
   732	        msg = "insufficient_unpacked" if require_unpacked else "insufficient_stock"
   733	        raise ValueError(msg)

   736	async def reverse_packaging_convert(
   737	    session: AsyncSession,
   738	    *,
   739	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   740	    product_id: uuid.UUID,
   741	    storage_location_id: uuid.UUID,
   742	    quantity: int,
   743	) -> None:
   744	    """Вернуть qty из упаковано в не упаковано в той же ячейке."""
   745	    if quantity < 1:
   746	        msg = "quantity must be positive"
   747	        raise ValueError(msg)
   748	    loc = await session.get(StorageLocation, storage_location_id)
   749	    if loc is None or loc.tenant_id != tenant_id:
   750	        msg = "storage location not found"
   751	        raise ValueError(msg)
   752	    prod = await session.get(Product, product_id)
   753	    if prod is None or prod.tenant_id != tenant_id:
   754	        msg = "product not found"
   755	        raise ValueError(msg)
   756	    unpacked = InventoryBalance.quantity_unpacked
   757	    packed = InventoryBalance.quantity_packed
   758	    updated_balance_id = await session.scalar(
   759	        update(InventoryBalance)
   760	        .where(
   761	            InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   762	            InventoryBalance.product_id == product_id,
   763	            InventoryBalance.storage_location_id == storage_location_id,
   764	            InventoryBalance.container_kind.is_(None),
   765	            InventoryBalance.container_id.is_(None),
   766	            packed >= quantity,
   767	        )
   768	        .values(
   769	            quantity_unpacked=unpacked + quantity,
   770	            quantity_packed=packed - quantity,
   771	            quantity=unpacked + packed,
   772	            updated_at=datetime.now(UTC),
   773	        )
   774	        .returning(InventoryBalance.id)
   775	        .execution_options(synchronize_session=False)
   776	    )
   777	    if updated_balance_id is None:
   778	        msg = "insufficient_packed"
   779	        raise ValueError(msg)

   782	async def apply_inbound_receive(
   783	    session: AsyncSession,
   784	    *,
   785	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   786	    product_id: uuid.UUID,
   787	    storage_location_id: uuid.UUID,

   790	    inbound_intake_line_id: uuid.UUID,
   791	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   792	) -> None:
   793	    """Приход по строке приёмки (положительный delta)."""
   794	    if quantity <= 0:

   810	    session: AsyncSession,

   812	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,

   820	    if quantity <= 0:

   825	        tenant_id=tenant_id,

   835	async def apply_putaway_from_sorting(
   836	    session: AsyncSession,
   837	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   838	    *,
   839	    from_storage_location_id: uuid.UUID,
   840	    to_storage_location_id: uuid.UUID,
   841	    product_id: uuid.UUID,
   842	    quantity: int,
   843	    inbound_intake_line_id: uuid.UUID,
   844	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   845	) -> None:
   846	    """Перемещение из зоны сортировки в ячейку хранения (привязка к строке приёмки)."""
   847	    if quantity < 1:
   848	        msg = "quantity must be positive"
   849	        raise ValueError(msg)
   850	    if from_storage_location_id == to_storage_location_id:
   851	        msg = "from and to must differ"
   852	        raise ValueError(msg)
   853	
   854	    loc_from = await session.get(StorageLocation, from_storage_location_id)
   855	    loc_to = await session.get(StorageLocation, to_storage_location_id)
   856	    if (
   857	        loc_from is None
   858	        or loc_to is None
   859	        or loc_from.tenant_id != tenant_id
   860	        or loc_to.tenant_id != tenant_id
   861	    ):
   862	        msg = "storage location not found"
   863	        raise ValueError(msg)
   864	    if loc_from.warehouse_id != loc_to.warehouse_id:
   865	        msg = "locations must be in the same warehouse"
   866	        raise ValueError(msg)
   867	
   868	    avail = await available_quantity_at_location(
   869	        session, tenant_id, product_id, from_storage_location_id
   870	    )
   871	    if avail < quantity:
   872	        msg = "insufficient stock"
   873	        raise ValueError(msg)
   874	
   875	    group_id = uuid.uuid4()
   876	    await record_movement_and_adjust_balance(
   877	        session,
   878	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   879	        product_id=product_id,
   880	        storage_location_id=from_storage_location_id,
   881	        quantity_delta=-quantity,
   882	        movement_type=MOVEMENT_TYPE_STOCK_TRANSFER_OUT,
   883	        transfer_group_id=group_id,
   884	        inbound_intake_line_id=inbound_intake_line_id,
   885	        actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   886	    )
   887	    await record_movement_and_adjust_balance(
   888	        session,
   889	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   890	        product_id=product_id,
   891	        storage_location_id=to_storage_location_id,
   892	        quantity_delta=quantity,
   893	        movement_type=MOVEMENT_TYPE_STOCK_TRANSFER_IN,
   894	        transfer_group_id=group_id,
   895	        inbound_intake_line_id=inbound_intake_line_id,
   896	        actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   897	    )

   601	def _checks_to_payload(checks: list[DeliveryCheck]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   602	    return [
   603	        {
   604	            "code": check.code,
   605	            "message": check.message,
   606	            "ok": check.ok,
   607	            "order_id": str(check.order_id) if check.order_id is not None else None,
   608	        }
   609	        for check in checks
   610	    ]

   613	def _validate_checks_pass(checks: list[DeliveryCheck]) -> None:
   614	    for check in checks:
   615	        if not check.ok:
   616	            raise FbsShipmentError(check.code)

   619	async def _sync_and_validate_deliver(
   620	    session: AsyncSession,
   621	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   622	    supply: FbsSupply,
   623	    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
   624	    token: str,
   625	    *,
   626	    confirmed_preflight_version: str | None = None,
   627	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   628	) -> tuple[list[FbsOrder], bool]:
   629	    orders = await _sync_supply_orders_from_wb(
   630	        session,
   631	        tenant_id,
   632	        supply,
   633	        http_client,
   634	        token,
   635	        actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   636	    )
   637	    cargo_qr_ready = True
   638	    if supply.delivery_type == FBS_DELIVERY_TYPE_PVZ:
   639	        cargo_qr_ready = await pvz_svc.supply_has_ready_cargo_place_qrs(session, tenant_id, supply)
   640	    box_readiness = await packing_box_svc.get_delivery_box_readiness(
   641	        session, tenant_id, supply.id, orders
   642	    )
   643	    if confirmed_preflight_version is not None:
   644	        current_version = _compute_preflight_version(
   645	            supply,
   646	            orders,
   647	            cargo_qr_ready=cargo_qr_ready,
   648	            has_physical_boxes=box_readiness.has_physical_boxes,
   649	            without_distribution=box_readiness.without_distribution,
   650	            unassigned_packed_order_ids=box_readiness.unassigned_packed_order_ids,
   651	        )
   652	        if current_version != confirmed_preflight_version:
   653	            raise FbsShipmentError(
   654	                "stale_preflight",
   655	                message="Чек-лист устарел — обновите preflight.",
   656	                context={
   657	                    "current_version": current_version,
   658	                    "confirmed_preflight_version": confirmed_preflight_version,
   659	                },
   660	                http_status=409,
   661	            )
   662	    checks = _build_delivery_checks(
   663	        supply,
   664	        orders,
   665	        cargo_qr_ready=cargo_qr_ready,
   666	        has_physical_boxes=box_readiness.has_physical_boxes,
   667	        without_distribution=box_readiness.without_distribution,
   668	        unassigned_packed_order_ids=box_readiness.unassigned_packed_order_ids,
   669	    )
   670	    _validate_checks_pass(checks)
   671	    return orders, cargo_qr_ready
   672	
   673	
   674	def _meta_validation_context(exc: WildberriesBusinessError) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   675	    return [
   676	        {
   677	            "order_id": item.order_id,
   678	            "key": item.key,
   679	            "value": item.value,
   680	            "decision": item.decision,
   681	            "reason": item.reason,
   682	        }
   683	        for item in exc.meta_validation
   684	    ]
   685	
   686	
   687	async def preflight_delivery(
   688	    session: AsyncSession,
   689	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   690	    supply_id: uuid.UUID,
   691	    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
   692	    *,
   693	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   694	) -> DeliveryPreflightResult:
   695	    supply = await _get_supply_read(session, tenant_id, supply_id, with_trbxes=True)
   696	    if supply is None:
   697	        raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
   698	    if supply.delivery_type not in _DELIVER_ALLOWED_DELIVERY_TYPES:
   699	        raise FbsShipmentError("wrong_delivery_type")
   700	
   701	    if supply.marketplace == "ozon":
   702	        await _ozon_credentials(session, tenant_id, supply.seller_id)
   703	        orders = await _load_supply_orders_read(session, tenant_id, supply.id)
   704	        checks = _build_delivery_checks(
   705	            supply,
   706	            orders,
   707	            cargo_qr_ready=True,
   708	            boxes_required=False,
   709	        )
   710	        checked_at = datetime.now(UTC)
   711	        version = _compute_preflight_version(
   712	            supply,
   713	            orders,
   714	            cargo_qr_ready=True,
   715	            has_physical_boxes=False,
   716	            without_distribution=False,
   717	            unassigned_packed_order_ids=frozenset(),
   718	        )
   719	        return DeliveryPreflightResult(
   720	            can_deliver=all(check.ok for check in checks),
   721	            version=version,
   722	            checked_at=checked_at,
   723	            checks=tuple(checks),
   724	        )
   725	
   726	    token = await _require_marketplace_token(session, tenant_id, supply.seller_id)
   727	    orders = await _sync_supply_orders_from_wb(
   728	        session,
   729	        tenant_id,
   730	        supply,
   731	        http_client,
   732	        token,
   733	        actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   734	    )
   735	    cargo_qr_ready = True
   736	    if supply.delivery_type == FBS_DELIVERY_TYPE_PVZ:
   737	        cargo_qr_ready = await pvz_svc.supply_has_ready_cargo_place_qrs(session, tenant_id, supply)
   738	    box_readiness = await packing_box_svc.get_delivery_box_readiness(
   739	        session, tenant_id, supply.id, orders
   740	    )
   741	
   742	    checks = _build_delivery_checks(
   743	        supply,
   744	        orders,
   745	        cargo_qr_ready=cargo_qr_ready,
   746	        has_physical_boxes=box_readiness.has_physical_boxes,
   747	        without_distribution=box_readiness.without_distribution,
   748	        unassigned_packed_order_ids=box_readiness.unassigned_packed_order_ids,
   749	    )
   750	    checked_at = datetime.now(UTC)
   751	    version = _compute_preflight_version(
   752	        supply,
   753	        orders,
   754	        cargo_qr_ready=cargo_qr_ready,
   755	        has_physical_boxes=box_readiness.has_physical_boxes,
   756	        without_distribution=box_readiness.without_distribution,
   757	        unassigned_packed_order_ids=box_readiness.unassigned_packed_order_ids,
   758	    )
   759	    can_deliver = all(check.ok for check in checks)
   760	    return DeliveryPreflightResult(
   761	        can_deliver=can_deliver,
   762	        version=version,
   763	        checked_at=checked_at,
   764	        checks=tuple(checks),
   765	    )
   766	
   767	
   768	def delivery_preflight_to_dict(result: DeliveryPreflightResult) -> dict[str, Any]:
   769	    return {
   770	        "can_deliver": result.can_deliver,
   771	        "version": result.version,
   772	        "checked_at": result.checked_at.isoformat(),
   773	        "checks": _checks_to_payload(list(result.checks)),
   774	    }
   775	
   776	
   777	async def _apply_local_delivered(
   778	    session: AsyncSession,
   779	    supply: FbsSupply,
   780	    orders: list[FbsOrder],
   781	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   782	) -> None:
   783	    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(session, supply, orders, actor_user_id)
   784	    now = datetime.now(UTC)
   785	    supply.status = FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_IN_DELIVERY
   786	    supply.delivered_at = now
   787	    for order in orders:
   788	        if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_PACKED:
   789	            order.status = FBS_ORDER_STATUS_IN_DELIVERY
   790	    await session.flush()
   791	
   792	
   793	async def _shipment_locations_by_order(
   794	    session: AsyncSession,
   795	    order_ids: list[uuid.UUID],
   796	) -> dict[uuid.UUID, tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID]]:
   797	    """Return order -> (product, physical shipment location), preferring packing."""
   798	    if not order_ids:
   799	        return {}
   800	    fulfillment_rows = (
   801	        await session.execute(
   802	            select(
   803	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.fbs_order_id,
   804	                PackagingTaskLine.product_id,
   805	                PackagingTaskLine.storage_location_id,
   806	            )
   807	            .join(
   808	                PackagingTaskLine,
   809	                PackagingTaskLine.id == FbsPackagingFulfillment.packaging_task_line_id,
   810	            )
   811	            .where(
   812	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.fbs_order_id.in_(order_ids),
   813	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.undone_at.is_(None),
   814	            )
   815	        )
   816	    ).all()
   817	    result = {
   818	        order_id: (product_id, storage_location_id)
   819	        for order_id, product_id, storage_location_id in fulfillment_rows
   820	    }
   821	    missing_ids = [order_id for order_id in order_ids if order_id not in result]
   822	    if not missing_ids:
   823	        return result
   824	    pick_rows = (
   825	        await session.execute(
   826	            select(
   827	                FbsOrderPick.fbs_order_id,
   828	                FbsOrderPick.product_id,
   829	                FbsOrderPick.sorting_storage_location_id,
   830	            ).where(
   831	                FbsOrderPick.fbs_order_id.in_(missing_ids),
   832	                FbsOrderPick.undone_at.is_(None),
   833	            )
   834	        )
   835	    ).all()
   836	    result.update(
   837	        {
   838	            order_id: (product_id, storage_location_id)
   839	            for order_id, product_id, storage_location_id in pick_rows
   840	        }
   841	    )
   842	    return result
   843	
   844	
   845	async def _write_off_delivered_orders_once(
   846	    session: AsyncSession,
   847	    supply: FbsSupply,
   848	    orders: list[FbsOrder],
   849	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   850	) -> None:
   851	    """Create exactly one physical write-off recipe per confirmed FBS order."""
   852	    active_orders = [
   853	        order
   854	        for order in orders
   855	        if order.status != FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED
   856	        and (order.product_id is not None or order.marketplace == "ozon")
   857	    ]
   858	    order_ids = [order.id for order in active_orders]
   859	    existing_ledgers = {
   860	        ledger.fbs_order_id: ledger

exec

   625	@pytest.mark.asyncio
   626	async def test_ozon_repeat_sync_replaces_changed_quantity_and_rereserves(
   627	    db_session: AsyncSession,
   628	) -> None:
   629	    """TC-S03-OZON-026: changed Ozon composition replaces its exact reserve without duplicates."""
   630	    order, products = await _sync_ozon_posting_with_products(
   631	        db_session,

   668	                            "name": "Футболка",
   669	                            "quantity": 3,
   670	                        }
   671	                    ],
   672	                }
   673	            ]
   674	        )
   675	    )
   676	
   677	    await ozon_sync_svc.sync_ozon_orders(
   678	        db_session,
   679	        order.tenant_id,

   690	    )

   711	    assert order.reserve_status == "reserved"

   714	async def _seed_physical_ozon_packaging(
   715	    session: AsyncSession,
   716	    order: FbsOrder,
   717	    supply: FbsSupply,
   718	    product_quantities: list[tuple[Product, int]],
   719	) -> None:
   720	    """Give provider-contract tests the physical packing state delivery requires."""
   721	    now = datetime.now(UTC)
   722	    task = PackagingTask(
   723	        tenant_id=order.tenant_id,
   724	        warehouse_id=supply.warehouse_id,
   725	        status="done",
   726	    )
   727	    session.add(task)
   728	    await session.flush()
   729	    supply.packaging_task_id = task.id
   730	    packed_units: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   731	    first_line: PackagingTaskLine | None = None
   732	    for index, (product, quantity) in enumerate(product_quantities):
   733	        location = StorageLocation(
   734	            tenant_id=order.tenant_id,
   735	            warehouse_id=supply.warehouse_id,
   736	            code=f"OZON-PACK-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
   737	            barcode=f"OZON-PACK-BC-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
   738	        )
   739	        session.add(location)
   740	        await session.flush()
   741	        line = PackagingTaskLine(
   742	            task_id=task.id,
   743	            product_id=product.id,
   744	            storage_location_id=location.id,
   745	            qty_total=quantity,
   746	            qty_packed_in_task=quantity,
   747	        )
   748	        session.add_all(
   749	            [
   750	                line,
   751	                InventoryBalance(
   752	                    tenant_id=order.tenant_id,
   753	                    product_id=product.id,
   754	                    storage_location_id=location.id,
   755	                    quantity=quantity,
   756	                    quantity_unpacked=0,
   757	                    quantity_packed=quantity,
   758	                ),
   759	            ]
   760	        )
   761	        await session.flush()
   762	        first_line = first_line or line
   763	        packed_units.extend(
   764	            {
   765	                "product_id": str(product.id),
   766	                "packaging_task_line_id": str(line.id),
   767	                "storage_location_id": str(location.id),
   768	                "idempotency_key": f"ozon-provider-pack-{index}-{unit}",
   769	                "packed_at": now.isoformat(),
   770	            }
   771	            for unit in range(quantity)
   772	        )
   773	    assert first_line is not None
   774	    session.add(
   775	        FbsPackagingFulfillment(
   776	            tenant_id=order.tenant_id,
   777	            fbs_order_id=order.id,
   778	            packaging_task_id=task.id,
   779	            packaging_task_line_id=first_line.id,
   780	            fulfilled_at=now,
   781	            pack_idempotency_key=f"ozon-provider-pack-{uuid.uuid4()}",
   782	            ozon_packed_units_json=packed_units,
   783	        )
   784	    )
   785	    await session.commit()
   786

   941	                        "requirements": {"products_requiring_country": []},
   942	                    }
   943	                },
   944	                responses["/v3/posting/fbs/get"],
   945	            ]
   946	        },
   947	    )
   948	
   949	    await shipment_svc.deliver_supply(
   950	        db_session,
   951	        tenant.id,
   952	        supply.id,
   953	        AsyncMock(),
   954	        idempotency_key=f"ozon-country-{uuid.uuid4()}",
   955	        actor_user_id=None,
   956	        ozon_provider=OzonMarketplaceProvider(transport=transport),
   957	    )
   958	
   959	    paths = [path for path, _ in transport.endpoint_calls]
   960	    assert (
   961	        paths.index("/v2/posting/fbs/product/country/list")
   962	        < paths.index("/v2/posting/fbs/product/country/set")
   963	        < paths.index("/v4/posting/fbs/ship")
   964	    )
   965	    country_payload = next(
   966	        payload
   967	        for path, payload in transport.endpoint_calls
   968	        if path == "/v2/posting/fbs/product/country/set"
   969	    )
   970	    assert country_payload == {
   971	        "posting_number": order.external_order_id,
   972	        "product_id": 3001,
   973	        "country_iso_code": "RU",
   974	    }

     1	"""PR #140 review: physical shipment follows the packed Ozon composition."""

    19	    FBS_ORDER_STATUS_PACKED,
    20	    MAPPING_STATUS_MAPPED,
    21	    PACK_STATUS_PACKED,
    22	    RESERVE_STATUS_NO_STOCK,
    23	    FbsOrder,
    24	    FbsOrderProduct,

    45	@pytest_asyncio.fixture
    46	async def db_session() -> AsyncIterator[AsyncSession]:
    47	    async with engine.begin() as connection:
    48	        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
    49	        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
    50	    async with SessionLocal() as session:
    51	        yield session
    52	    async with engine.begin() as connection:
    53	        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
    54	
    55	
    56	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    57	class _ShipmentCase:
    58	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID
    59	    supply: FbsSupply
    60	    order: FbsOrder
    61	    product_ids: tuple[uuid.UUID, ...]
    62	    location_ids: tuple[uuid.UUID, ...]
    63	    initial_quantity: int
    64	
    65	
    66	async def _seed_packed_ozon_order(
    67	    session: AsyncSession,
    68	    quantities: tuple[int, ...],
    69	) -> _ShipmentCase:
    70	    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
    71	    tenant = Tenant(name="PR140 shipment", slug=f"pr140-shipment-{suffix}")
    72	    seller = Seller(tenant=tenant, name="Seller")
    73	    warehouse = Warehouse(
    74	        tenant=tenant,
    75	        name="Ozon FBS",
    76	        code=f"ozon-pr140-{suffix}",
    77	    )
    78	    task = PackagingTask(tenant=tenant, warehouse=warehouse, status="done")
    79	    supply = FbsSupply(
    80	        tenant=tenant,
    81	        seller=seller,
    82	        warehouse=warehouse,
    83	        marketplace="ozon",
    84	        wb_supply_id=f"PENDING-{suffix}",
    85	        name="Packed Ozon supply",
    86	        status=FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_PACKED,
    87	        delivery_type=FBS_DELIVERY_TYPE_WAREHOUSE_SC,
    88	    )
    89	    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    90	    order = FbsOrder(
    91	        tenant=tenant,
    92	        seller=seller,
    93	        warehouse=warehouse,
    94	        supply=supply,
    95	        marketplace="ozon",
    96	        external_order_id=f"posting-{suffix}",
    97	        wb_order_id=-int(suffix, 16),
    98	        status=FBS_ORDER_STATUS_PACKED,
    99	        mapping_status=MAPPING_STATUS_MAPPED,
   100	        reserve_status=RESERVE_STATUS_NO_STOCK,
   101	        pack_status=PACK_STATUS_PACKED,
   102	        created_at_wb=now,
   103	        deadline_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
   104	    )
   105	    session.add_all([tenant, seller, warehouse, task, supply, order])
   106	    await session.flush()
   107	    supply.packaging_task_id = task.id
   108	
   109	    initial_quantity = 5
   110	    products: list[Product] = []
   111	    locations: list[StorageLocation] = []
   112	    lines: list[PackagingTaskLine] = []
   113	    for index, quantity in enumerate(quantities):
   114	        product = Product(
   115	            tenant=tenant,
   116	            seller=seller,
   117	            name=f"Product {index}",
   118	            sku_code=f"PR140-{suffix}-{index}",
   119	        )
   120	        location = StorageLocation(
   121	            tenant=tenant,
   122	            warehouse=warehouse,
   123	            code=f"CELL-{suffix}-{index}",
   124	            barcode=f"LOC-{suffix}-{index}",
   125	        )
   126	        session.add_all([product, location])
   127	        await session.flush()
   128	        line = PackagingTaskLine(
   129	            task=task,
   130	            product_id=product.id,
   131	            storage_location_id=location.id,
   132	            qty_total=quantity,
   133	            qty_packed_in_task=quantity,
   134	        )
   135	        position = FbsOrderProduct(
   136	            order=order,
   137	            product_id=product.id,
   138	            ozon_sku=10_000 + index,
   139	            offer_id=f"offer-{index}",
   140	            name=product.name,
   141	            quantity=quantity,
   142	            picked_quantity=quantity,
   143	            position_index=index,
   144	        )
   145	        balance = InventoryBalance(
   146	            tenant_id=tenant.id,
   147	            product_id=product.id,
   148	            storage_location_id=location.id,
   149	            quantity=initial_quantity,
   150	            quantity_unpacked=0,
   151	            quantity_packed=initial_quantity,
   152	        )
   153	        session.add_all([line, position, balance])
   154	        products.append(product)
   155	        locations.append(location)
   156	        lines.append(line)
   157	    await session.flush()
   158	    order.product_id = products[0].id
   159	
   160	    packed_units = [
   161	        {
   162	            "product_id": str(product.id),
   163	            "packaging_task_line_id": str(line.id),
   164	            "storage_location_id": str(location.id),
   165	            "idempotency_key": f"packed-{product.id}-{unit}",
   166	            "packed_at": now.isoformat(),
   167	        }
   168	        for product, location, line, quantity in zip(
   169	            products, locations, lines, quantities, strict=True
   170	        )
   171	        for unit in range(quantity)
   172	    ]
   173	    session.add(
   174	        FbsPackagingFulfillment(
   175	            tenant_id=tenant.id,
   176	            fbs_order_id=order.id,
   177	            packaging_task_id=task.id,
   178	            packaging_task_line_id=lines[0].id,
   179	            fulfilled_at=now,
   180	            pack_idempotency_key=f"packed-{suffix}",
   181	            ozon_packed_units_json=packed_units,
   182	        )
   183	    )
   184	    await session.commit()
   185	
   186	    loaded_order = (
   187	        await session.execute(
   188	            select(FbsOrder)
   189	            .where(FbsOrder.id == order.id)
   190	            .options(selectinload(FbsOrder.product_positions))
   191	        )
   192	    ).scalar_one()
   193	    loaded_supply = await session.get(FbsSupply, supply.id)
   194	    assert loaded_supply is not None
   195	    return _ShipmentCase(
   196	        tenant_id=tenant.id,
   197	        supply=loaded_supply,
   198	        order=loaded_order,
   199	        product_ids=tuple(product.id for product in products),
   200	        location_ids=tuple(location.id for location in locations),
   201	        initial_quantity=initial_quantity,
   202	    )
   203	
   204	
   205	async def _balances(session: AsyncSession, case: _ShipmentCase) -> dict[uuid.UUID, int]:
   206	    rows = (
   207	        await session.execute(
   208	            select(InventoryBalance).where(
   209	                InventoryBalance.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
   210	                InventoryBalance.product_id.in_(case.product_ids),
   211	            )
   212	        )
   213	    ).scalars()
   214	    return {row.product_id: int(row.quantity) for row in rows}
   215	
   216	
   217	@pytest.mark.asyncio
   218	async def test_ozon_two_products_are_written_off_from_their_packed_locations(
   219	    db_session: AsyncSession,
   220	) -> None:
   221	    # TC-NEW-FBS-SHIPMENT-COMPOSITION-001
   222	    case = await _seed_packed_ozon_order(db_session, (1, 1))
   223	
   224	    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
   225	
   226	    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
   227	        product_id: case.initial_quantity - 1 for product_id in case.product_ids
   228	    }
   229	    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
   230	        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
   231	            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
   232	        )
   233	    )
   234	    assert ledger is not None
   235	    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
   236	    assert {
   237	        (position["product_id"], position["storage_location_id"], position["quantity"])
   238	        for position in ledger.ozon_positions_json or []
   239	    } == {
   240	        (str(product_id), str(location_id), 1)
   241	        for product_id, location_id in zip(
   242	            case.product_ids, case.location_ids, strict=True
   243	        )
   244	    }

   269	@pytest.mark.asyncio
   270	async def test_ozon_cancellation_restores_every_written_off_position(
   271	    db_session: AsyncSession,
   272	) -> None:
   273	    # TC-NEW-FBS-SHIPMENT-COMPOSITION-003
   274	    case = await _seed_packed_ozon_order(db_session, (2, 3))
   275	    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
   276	
   277	    await _apply_status(db_session, case.order, "cancelled")
   278	    await db_session.flush()
   279	
   280	    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
   281	        product_id: case.initial_quantity for product_id in case.product_ids
   282	    }
   283	    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
   284	        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
   285	            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
   286	        )
   287	    )
   288	    assert ledger is not None
   289	    assert ledger.reversed_at is not None
   290	    movements = list(
   291	        (
   292	            await db_session.execute(
   293	                select(InventoryMovement).where(
   294	                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
   295	                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
   296	                )
   297	            )
   298	        )
   299	        .scalars()
   300	        .all()
   301	    )
   302	    assert sorted(int(movement.quantity_delta) for movement in movements) == [-3, -2, 2, 3]

   131	            fulfilled_at=packed_at,
   132	            pack_idempotency_key=unit_key,
   133	            ozon_packed_units_json=[packed_unit],
   134	        )
   135	    else:
   136	        fulfillment.ozon_packed_units_json = [*packed_units(fulfillment), packed_unit]
   137	    if packed_quantity(fulfillment) == required_quantity(target_order):
   138	        target_order.pack_status = PACK_STATUS_PACKED
   139	        target_order.packed_at = packed_at
   140	    else:
   141	        target_order.pack_status = PACK_STATUS_PENDING
   142	        target_order.packed_at = None
   143	    return fulfillment
   144	
   145	
   146	async def write_off_order(
   147	    session: AsyncSession,
   148	    *,
   149	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   150	    order: FbsOrder,
   151	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   152	) -> FbsShipmentReversalLedger:
   153	    """Write off every packed Ozon unit and preserve its exact reversal recipe."""
   154	    fulfillment = await active_order_fulfillment(session, order.id)
   155	    units = packed_units(fulfillment)
   156	    if not units or not await order_pack_complete(session, order):
   157	        raise OzonPackagingError("missing_fbs_packaging_location")
   158	    grouped: dict[tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID], int] = defaultdict(int)
   159	    for unit in units:
   160	        try:
   161	            product_id = uuid.UUID(unit["product_id"])
   162	            storage_location_id = uuid.UUID(unit["storage_location_id"])
   163	        except (KeyError, ValueError) as exc:
   164	            raise OzonPackagingError("invalid_ozon_packaging_fulfillment") from exc
   165	        grouped[(product_id, storage_location_id)] += 1
   166	    positions_json = [
   167	        {
   168	            "product_id": str(product_id),
   169	            "storage_location_id": str(storage_location_id),
   170	            "quantity": quantity,
   171	        }
   172	        for (product_id, storage_location_id), quantity in sorted(
   173	            grouped.items(), key=lambda item: (str(item[0][0]), str(item[0][1]))
   174	        )
   175	    ]
   176	    first_product_id, first_storage_location_id = next(iter(grouped))
   177	    ledger = FbsShipmentReversalLedger(
   178	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   179	        fbs_order_id=order.id,
   180	        product_id=first_product_id,
   181	        storage_location_id=first_storage_location_id,
   182	        quantity=sum(grouped.values()),
   183	        ozon_positions_json=positions_json,
   184	    )
   185	    session.add(ledger)
   186	    await session.flush()
   187	    first_movement_id: uuid.UUID | None = None
   188	    for (product_id, storage_location_id), quantity in grouped.items():
   189	        movement = await inv_svc.apply_fbs_supply_write_off(
   190	            session,
   191	            tenant_id=tenant_id,
   192	            product_id=product_id,
   193	            storage_location_id=storage_location_id,
   194	            quantity=quantity,
   195	            actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   196	        )
   197	        await session.flush()
   198	        if first_movement_id is None:
   199	            first_movement_id = movement.id
   200	    ledger.shipment_movement_id = first_movement_id
   201	    await session.flush()
   202	    return ledger

   845	async def _write_off_delivered_orders_once(
   846	    session: AsyncSession,
   847	    supply: FbsSupply,
   848	    orders: list[FbsOrder],
   849	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   850	) -> None:
   851	    """Create exactly one physical write-off recipe per confirmed FBS order."""
   852	    active_orders = [
   853	        order
   854	        for order in orders
   855	        if order.status != FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED
   856	        and (order.product_id is not None or order.marketplace == "ozon")
   857	    ]
   858	    order_ids = [order.id for order in active_orders]
   859	    existing_ledgers = {
   860	        ledger.fbs_order_id: ledger
   861	        for ledger in (
   862	            (
   863	                await session.execute(
   864	                    select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger)
   865	                    .where(
   866	                        FbsShipmentReversalLedger.tenant_id == supply.tenant_id,
   867	                        FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id.in_(order_ids),
   868	                    )
   869	                    .with_for_update()
   870	                )
   871	            )
   872	            .scalars()
   873	            .all()
   874	        )
   875	    }
   876	    locations = await _shipment_locations_by_order(session, order_ids)
   877	    fallback_location_id: uuid.UUID | None = None
   878	
   879	    for order in active_orders:
   880	        ledger = existing_ledgers.get(order.id)
   881	        if ledger is None and order.marketplace == "ozon" and order.product_positions:
   882	            try:
   883	                ledger = await write_off_ozon_order(
   884	                    session,
   885	                    tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
   886	                    order=order,
   887	                    actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   888	                )
   889	            except OzonPackagingError as exc:
   890	                raise FbsShipmentError(str(exc), http_status=409) from exc
   891	            existing_ledgers[order.id] = ledger
   892	        if ledger is None:
   893	            if order.product_id is None:
   894	                raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_product_missing", http_status=409)
   895	            shipment_location = locations.get(order.id)
   896	            if shipment_location is not None:
   897	                fulfilled_product_id, storage_location_id = shipment_location
   898	                if fulfilled_product_id != order.product_id:
   899	                    raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_product_mismatch", http_status=409)
   900	            else:
   901	                if fallback_location_id is None:
   902	                    fallback_location = await get_or_create_sorting_location(
   903	                        session,
   904	                        supply.tenant_id,
   905	                        supply.warehouse_id,
   906	                    )
   907	                    fallback_location_id = fallback_location.id
   908	                storage_location_id = fallback_location_id
   909	            ledger = FbsShipmentReversalLedger(
   910	                tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
   911	                fbs_order_id=order.id,
   912	                product_id=order.product_id,
   913	                storage_location_id=storage_location_id,
   914	                quantity=1,
   915	            )
   916	            session.add(ledger)
   917	            await session.flush()
   918	            existing_ledgers[order.id] = ledger
   919	
   920	        if ledger.reversed_at is not None:
   921	            raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_already_reversed", http_status=409)
   922	        if ledger.shipment_movement_id is None:
   923	            movement = await inventory_svc.apply_fbs_supply_write_off(
   924	                session,
   925	                tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
   926	                product_id=ledger.product_id,
   927	                storage_location_id=ledger.storage_location_id,
   928	                quantity=int(ledger.quantity),
   929	                actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   930	            )
   931	            await session.flush()
   932	            ledger.shipment_movement_id = movement.id
   933	        await _release_reservation(session, order)
   934	    await session.flush()
   935

   937	async def _fetch_supply_qr_after_deliver(
   938	    session: AsyncSession,
   939	    supply: FbsSupply,
   940	    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
   941	    token: str,
   942	) -> None:
   943	    # WB issues a supply QR (GET /api/v3/supplies/{id}/barcode) regardless of
   944	    # delivery_type — verified against the WB API for both warehouse_sc and pvz
   945	    # supplies on 2026-08-17. Do not re-add a delivery_type gate here.
   960	            wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
   961	            event="fbs shipment WB supply QR fetch failed",
   962	        ) from exc
   963	    supply.barcode_file = _save_barcode_png(supply.id, png_bytes)
   964	    try:
   965	        await upsert_supply_qr_asset_from_bytes(
   966	            session,
   967	            tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
   968	            supply=supply,
   969	            png_bytes=png_bytes,
   970	        )
   971	    except FbsPrintAssetStorageError as exc:
   972	        raise FbsShipmentError(exc.code) from exc
   973	    await session.flush()
   974	
   975	
   976	async def _persist_confirmed_delivery(
   977	    session: AsyncSession,
   978	    supply: FbsSupply,
   979	    orders: list[FbsOrder],
   980	    operation: Any,
   981	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   982	) -> None:
   983	    """Durably checkpoint marketplace delivery before fetching its optional QR asset."""
   984	    await _apply_local_delivered(session, supply, orders, actor_user_id)
   985	    await mark_deliver_operation_confirmed(
   986	        session,
   987	        operation,
   988	        wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
   989	        local_supply_id=supply.id,
   990	    )
   991	    # The route only commits selected errors.  A barcode download failure must
   992	    # therefore not roll back the already confirmed WB delivery.
   993	    await session.commit()

  1014	async def _store_ozon_supply_qr(
  1015	    session: AsyncSession,
  1016	    supply: FbsSupply,
  1017	    provider: OzonMarketplaceProvider,
  1018	    *,
  1019	    client_id: str,
  1020	    api_key: str,
  1021	) -> bytes:
  1022	    external_supply_id = supply.external_supply_id or supply.wb_supply_id
  1023	    try:
  1024	        png_bytes = await provider.fetch_supply_qr(
  1025	            client_id=client_id,
  1026	            api_key=api_key,
  1027	            supply_id=external_supply_id,
  1028	        )
  1029	    except MarketplaceProviderError as exc:
  1030	        raise FbsShipmentError(
  1031	            exc.code,
  1032	            message=provider_error_message(exc),
  1033	            retryable=exc.status_code in {429, 500, 502, 503, 504},
  1034	            http_status=exc.status_code or 502,
  1035	        ) from exc
  1036	    if not png_bytes:
  1037	        raise FbsShipmentError(
  1038	            "ozon_supply_qr_missing",
  1039	            message="Ozon не вернул QR поставки.",
  1040	            http_status=502,
  1041	        )
  1042	    try:
  1043	        await upsert_supply_qr_asset_from_bytes(
  1044	            session,
  1045	            tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
  1046	            supply=supply,
  1047	            png_bytes=png_bytes,
  1048	        )
  1049	    except FbsPrintAssetStorageError as exc:
  1050	        raise FbsShipmentError(exc.code) from exc
  1051	    await session.flush()
  1052	    return png_bytes

  1055	async def _deliver_ozon_supply(
  1056	    session: AsyncSession,
  1057	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
  1058	    supply_id: uuid.UUID,
  1059	    *,
  1060	    idempotency_key: str,
  1061	    request_hash: str,
  1062	    existing: Any,
  1063	    provider: OzonMarketplaceProvider | None,
  1064	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
  1065	) -> FbsSupply:
  1066	    supply = await _get_supply_for_update(
  1067	        session,
  1068	        tenant_id,
  1069	        supply_id,
  1070	        with_trbxes=True,
  1071	    )
  1072	    if supply is None:
  1073	        raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
  1074	    if supply.delivery_type not in _DELIVER_ALLOWED_DELIVERY_TYPES:
  1075	        raise FbsShipmentError("wrong_delivery_type")
  1076	
  1077	    client_id, api_key = await _ozon_credentials(
  1078	        session,
  1079	        tenant_id,
  1080	        supply.seller_id,
  1081	    )
  1082	    if existing is not None:
  1083	        if existing.request_hash and existing.request_hash != request_hash:
  1084	            raise FbsShipmentError("idempotency_key_reused", http_status=409)
  1085	        if existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED:
  1086	            return supply
  1087	        if existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING:
  1088	            raise FbsShipmentError(
  1089	                "operation_in_progress",
  1090	                message="Передача в Ozon уже выполняется.",
  1091	                retryable=True,
  1092	                http_status=503,
  1093	            )
  1094	        raise FbsShipmentError(
  1095	            existing.error_code or "operation_failed",
  1096	            message="Предыдущая передача Ozon завершилась ошибкой; слепой повтор запрещён.",
  1097	            http_status=409,
  1098	        )
  1099	
  1100	    if supply.status in _DELIVER_BLOCKED_SUPPLY_STATUSES:
  1101	        raise FbsShipmentError("supply_bad_status", http_status=409)
  1102	    orders = await _load_locked_supply_orders(session, tenant_id, supply.id)
  1103	    _validate_checks_pass(
  1104	        _build_delivery_checks(
  1105	            supply,
  1106	            orders,
  1107	            cargo_qr_ready=True,
  1108	            boxes_required=False,
  1109	        )
  1110	    )
  1111	    operation = existing or await create_pending_deliver_operation(
  1112	        session,
  1113	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
  1114	        seller_id=supply.seller_id,
  1115	        idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
  1116	        request_hash=request_hash,
  1117	        local_supply_id=supply.id,
  1118	        confirmed_preflight_version=None,
  1119	    )
  1120	    if provider is None:
  1121	        await mark_operation_failed(
  1122	            session,
  1123	            operation,
  1124	            error_code="ozon_live_handoff_blocked",
  1125	            local_supply_id=supply.id,
  1180	                supply=supply,
  1181	                png_bytes=result.barcode_bytes,
  1182	            )
  1183	        except FbsPrintAssetStorageError as exc:
  1184	            raise FbsShipmentError(exc.code) from exc
  1185	    await _persist_confirmed_delivery(session, supply, orders, operation, actor_user_id)
  1186	    return supply
  1187	
  1188	
  1189	async def deliver_supply(
  1190	    session: AsyncSession,
  1191	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
  1192	    supply_id: uuid.UUID,
  1193	    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
  1194	    *,
  1195	    idempotency_key: str,
  1196	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
  1197	    confirmed_preflight_version: str | None = None,
  1198	    ozon_provider: OzonMarketplaceProvider | None = None,
  1199	) -> FbsSupply:
  1200	    if not idempotency_key.strip():
  1201	        raise FbsShipmentError("missing_idempotency_key")
  1202	
  1203	    supply_read = await _get_supply_read(session, tenant_id, supply_id, with_trbxes=True)
  1204	    if supply_read is None:
  1205	        raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
  1206	
  1207	    request_hash = request_hash_for_deliver(
  1208	        supply_id=supply_id,
  1209	        confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
  1210	    )
  1211	    existing = await get_deliver_operation_by_idempotency(
  1212	        session, supply_read.seller_id, idempotency_key
  1213	    )
  1214	    if supply_read.marketplace == "ozon":
  1215	        return await _deliver_ozon_supply(
  1216	            session,
  1217	            tenant_id,
  1218	            supply_id,
  1219	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
  1220	            request_hash=request_hash,
  1221	            existing=existing,
  1222	            provider=ozon_provider,
  1223	            actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
  1224	        )
  1225	    if existing is not None:
  1226	        if (
  1227	            existing.request_hash
  1228	            and existing.request_hash != request_hash
  1229	            and existing.state != WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING_CONFIRMATION
  1230	        ):
  1231	            raise FbsShipmentError("idempotency_key_reused", http_status=409)
  1232	        if existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED:
  1233	            confirmed_supply = await _get_supply_for_update(
  1234	                session, tenant_id, supply_id, with_trbxes=True
  1235	            )
  1236	            if confirmed_supply is not None:
  1237	                token = await _require_marketplace_token(
  1238	                    session, tenant_id, confirmed_supply.seller_id
  1239	                )
  1240	                # A prior QR failure is recoverable through the same idempotent
  1241	                # request and must never trigger another WB deliver mutation.
  1242	                await _fetch_supply_qr_after_deliver(session, confirmed_supply, http_client, token)
  1243	                return confirmed_supply
  1244	        if existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING:
  1245	            raise FbsShipmentError(
  1246	                "operation_in_progress",
  1247	                message="Передача в доставку уже выполняется.",
  1248	                retryable=True,
  1249	                http_status=503,
  1250	            )
  1251	        if existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING_CONFIRMATION:
  1252	            token = await _require_marketplace_token(session, tenant_id, supply_read.seller_id)
  1253	            reconcile_state = await reconcile_supply_delivered(
  1254	                http_client,
  1255	                api_token=token,
  1256	                wb_supply_id=supply_read.wb_supply_id,
  1257	            )
  1258	            supply = await _get_supply_for_update(session, tenant_id, supply_id, with_trbxes=True)
  1259	            if supply is None:
  1260	                raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
  1261	            await _sync_and_validate_deliver(
  1262	                session,
  1263	                tenant_id,
  1264	                supply,
  1265	                http_client,
  1266	                token,
  1267	                confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
  1268	                actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
  1269	            )
  1270	            if reconcile_state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED:
  1271	                orders = await _load_locked_supply_orders(session, tenant_id, supply.id)
  1272	                await _persist_confirmed_delivery(session, supply, orders, existing, actor_user_id)
  1273	                await _fetch_supply_qr_after_deliver(session, supply, http_client, token)
  1274	                return supply
  1275

Код в этом worktree уже отличается от описанной боевой версии: здесь `write_off_order()` действительно записывает первый идентификатор движения в `FbsShipmentReversalLedger.shipment_movement_id`, а доставочный путь читает ту же таблицу и при заполненном поле второй раз не списывает. Это изменение пришло в базу текущего worktree коммитом `250fb180…`; я не буду выдавать его за свою правку. Дальше проверяю, закрывает ли оно именно последовательность «упаковка → доставка → отмена» и не скрывает ли многопозиционный Ozon второй дефект.

   519	    if qty < 1:
   520	        raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("invalid_qty")
   521

   537	    for index in range(qty):
   538	        unit_key = base_key if qty == 1 else f"{base_key}:{index}"
   539	        existing = await session.scalar(
   540	            select(FbsPackagingFulfillment)
   541	            .where(
   542	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.packaging_task_id == task.id,
   543	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.pack_idempotency_key == unit_key,
   544	            )
   545	            .options(selectinload(FbsPackagingFulfillment.order))
   546	        )
   547	        if existing is None and supply.marketplace == "ozon":
   548	            active_fulfillments = list(
   549	                (
   550	                    await session.execute(
   551	                        select(FbsPackagingFulfillment)
   552	                        .where(
   553	                            FbsPackagingFulfillment.packaging_task_id == task.id,
   554	                            FbsPackagingFulfillment.undone_at.is_(None),
   555	                        )
   556	                        .options(selectinload(FbsPackagingFulfillment.order))
   557	                    )
   558	                )
   559	                .scalars()
   560	                .all()
   561	            )
   562	            existing = next(
   563	                (
   564	                    fulfillment
   565	                    for fulfillment in active_fulfillments
   566	                    if any(
   567	                        unit.get("idempotency_key") == unit_key
   568	                        for unit in _ozon_packed_units(fulfillment)
   569	                    )
   570	                ),
   571	                None,
   572	            )
   573	        if existing is not None:
   574	            units.append(FbsPackUnitResult(existing, existing.order))
   575	            continue
   576

   581	                    session,
   582	                    supply,
   583	                    line.product_id,
   584	                    explicit_order_id=order_id,
   585	                )
   586	            except OzonPackagingError as exc:
   587	                raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError(str(exc)) from exc
   588	        else:
   589	            target_order = await _resolve_order_for_pack_unit(
   590	                session,
   591	                supply,
   592	                line.product_id,
   593	                explicit_order_id=order_id if index == 0 else None,
   594	            )
   595	
   596	        try:
   597	            await inv_svc.apply_packaging_convert(
   598	                session,
   599	                tenant_id=tenant_id,
   600	                product_id=line.product_id,
   601	                storage_location_id=line.storage_location_id,
   602	                quantity=1,
   603	                # Packaging uses the total physical balance in the cell.
   604	                require_unpacked=False,
   605	            )
   606	        except ValueError as exc:
   607	            if str(exc) == "insufficient_stock":
   608	                success, alt_location_code = await _try_deduct_from_alternative_sorting_location(
   609	                    session,
   610	                    tenant_id,
   611	                    line.product_id,

   626	                    )

   628	                    insufficient_msg = await _insufficient_stock_message(session, tenant_id, line)

   630	                    logger.warning(
   631	                        "fbs packing without stock: tenant=%s product=%s location=%s order=%s",
   632	                        tenant_id,
   633	                        line.product_id,
   634	                        line.storage_location_id,
   635	                        target_order.id,
   636	                    )
   637	            else:
   638	                raise
   639	
   640	        now = datetime.now(UTC)
   641	        if supply.marketplace == "ozon":
   642	            fulfillment = _record_ozon_pack_unit(
   643	                tenant_id=tenant_id,
   644	                target_order=target_order,
   645	                fulfillment=ozon_fulfillment,

   649	                unit_key=unit_key,
   650	                packed_at=now,
   651	            )

   653	                session.add(fulfillment)

   655	            fulfillment = FbsPackagingFulfillment(
   656	                tenant_id=tenant_id,
   657	                fbs_order_id=target_order.id,
   658	                packaging_task_id=task.id,

   662	                pack_idempotency_key=unit_key,
   663	            )

   665	            target_order.pack_status = PACK_STATUS_PACKED
   666	            target_order.packed_at = now
   667	        line.qty_packed_in_task = int(line.qty_packed_in_task) + 1
   668	        units.append(FbsPackUnitResult(fulfillment, target_order))
   669	
   670	    await session.flush()
   671	    return FbsPackProgressResult(units=units, warnings=warnings)

   674	async def _all_active_orders_fulfilled(
   675	    session: AsyncSession,
   676	    supply: FbsSupply,
   677	) -> bool:
   678	    for order in supply.orders:
   679	        if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED:
   680	            continue
   681	        if order.marketplace == "ozon":
   682	            if not await _ozon_order_pack_complete(session, order):
   683	                return False
   684	            continue
   685	        if order.product_id is None:
   686	            return False
   687	        if not await _order_has_active_fulfillment(session, order.id):
   688	            return False
   689	    return True

    53	class FbsCancellationError(Exception):
    54	    def __init__(
    55	        self,
    56	        code: str,
    57	        *,
    58	        message: str | None = None,
    59	        context: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    60	        retryable: bool = False,
    61	    ) -> None:
    62	        self.code = code
    63	        self.message = message or code
    64	        self.context = context or {}

    69	async def reverse_fbs_shipment_if_needed(
    70	    session: AsyncSession,
    71	    order: FbsOrder,
    72	    *,
    73	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
    74	) -> bool:
    75	    """Reverse one physically written-off unit exactly once; caller owns the order lock."""
    76	    stmt = (
    77	        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger)
    78	        .where(
    79	            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.tenant_id == order.tenant_id,
    80	            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == order.id,
    81	        )
    82	        .with_for_update()
    83	    )
    84	    ledger = (await session.execute(stmt)).scalar_one_or_none()
    85	    if (
    86	        ledger is None
    87	        or ledger.shipment_movement_id is None
    88	        or ledger.reversed_at is not None
    89	    ):
    90	        return False
    91	    from app.services import stock_direction_service
    92	
    93	    positions = list(ledger.ozon_positions_json or [])
    94	    if not positions:
    95	        positions = [
    96	            {
    97	                "product_id": str(ledger.product_id),
    98	                "storage_location_id": str(ledger.storage_location_id),
    99	                "quantity": int(ledger.quantity),
   100	            }
   101	        ]
   102	    reversal_movement = None
   103	    for position in positions:
   104	        product_id = uuid.UUID(str(position["product_id"]))
   105	        storage_location_id = uuid.UUID(str(position["storage_location_id"]))
   106	        quantity = int(str(position["quantity"]))
   107	        movement = await inv_svc.record_movement_and_adjust_balance(
   108	            session,
   109	            tenant_id=order.tenant_id,
   110	            product_id=product_id,
   111	            storage_location_id=storage_location_id,
   112	            quantity_delta=quantity,
   113	            movement_type=MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
   114	            actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   115	        )
   116	        if reversal_movement is None:
   117	            reversal_movement = movement
   118	        await stock_direction_service.restore_fbs_pool(
   119	            session,
   120	            order.tenant_id,
   121	            product_id,
   122	            quantity,
   123	        )
   124	    ledger.reversed_at = datetime.now(UTC)
   125	    await session.flush()
   126	    ledger.reversal_movement_id = reversal_movement.id if reversal_movement is not None else None
   127	    await session.flush()
   128	    return True

   130	
   131	def penalty_band_for_order(created_at_wb: datetime) -> str:
   132	    """WB cancel penalty window band (logged only in MVP)."""
   133	    now = datetime.now(tz=UTC)
   134	    created = (
   135	        created_at_wb if created_at_wb.tzinfo is not None else created_at_wb.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   136	    )
   137	    hours = (now - created).total_seconds() / 3600.0
   138	    if hours < 13:
   139	        return "lt13"
   140	    if hours < 18:
   141	        return "13_18"
   142	    if hours < 120:
   143	        return "18_120"
   144	    return "gt120"

   147	async def cancel_order(
   148	    session: AsyncSession,
   149	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   150	    order_id: uuid.UUID,
   151	    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
   152	    *,
   153	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   154	) -> FbsOrder:
   155	    stmt = (
   156	        select(FbsOrder)
   157	        .where(FbsOrder.id == order_id, FbsOrder.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   158	        .with_for_update()
   159	    )
   160	    res = await session.execute(stmt)
   161	    order = res.scalar_one_or_none()
   162	    if order is None:
   163	        raise FbsCancellationError("order_not_found")
   164	
   165	    if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED:
   166	        return order
   167	
   168	    if order.status in NON_CANCELLABLE_STATUSES:
   169	        raise FbsCancellationError("order_not_cancellable")
   170	
   171	    band = penalty_band_for_order(order.created_at_wb)
   172	    logger.info(
   173	        "fbs_cancel_penalty_band order_id=%s wb_order_id=%s band=%s",
   174	        order.id,
   175	        order.wb_order_id,
   176	        band,
   177	    )
   178	
   179	    try:
   180	        api_token = await _resolve_marketplace_api_token(session, tenant_id, order.seller_id)

    56	def _text(row: dict[str, Any], *keys: str) -> str | None:
    57	    for key in keys:
    58	        value = row.get(key)
    59	        if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
    60	            return value.strip()
    61	        if isinstance(value, int):
    62	            return str(value)
    63	    return None
    64	
    65	
    66	def _parse_datetime(value: object) -> datetime:
    67	    if isinstance(value, datetime):
    68	        return value if value.tzinfo is not None else value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    69	    if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
    70	        try:
    71	            parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value.strip().replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    72	            return parsed if parsed.tzinfo is not None else parsed.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    73	        except ValueError:
    74	            pass
    75	    return datetime.now(tz=UTC)
    76	
    77	
    78	def _legacy_numeric_order_id(external_order_id: str) -> int:
    79	    """Fill the legacy non-null WB id without sharing WB's positive id space."""
    80	    digest = hashlib.blake2b(external_order_id.encode("utf-8"), digest_size=8).digest()
    81	    return -(int.from_bytes(digest, "big", signed=False) & ((1 << 63) - 1)) or -1
    82	
    83	
    84	def _local_status(raw_status: str | None) -> str:
    85	    normalized = (raw_status or "").strip().lower()
    86	    if normalized in _OZON_NEW_STATUSES:
    87	        return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_NEW
    88	    if normalized in _OZON_DELIVERY_STATUSES:
    89	        return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_IN_DELIVERY
    90	    if normalized in _OZON_DONE_STATUSES:
    91	        return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_DONE
    92	    if normalized in _OZON_CANCELLED_STATUSES:
    93	        return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED
    94	    return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_EXTERNAL_PROCESSING
    95	
    96	
    97	async def _credentials(
    98	    session: AsyncSession,
    99	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   100	    seller_id: uuid.UUID,
   101	) -> tuple[str, str]:
   102	    return await MarketplaceAccountService(session).stored_credentials(tenant_id, seller_id)
   103	
   104	
   105	def _stock_error_code(error: MarketplaceProviderError) -> str:
   106	    if error.is_account_blocked:
   107	        return "ozon_account_blocked"
   108	    if error.status_code in {401, 403}:
   109	        return "ozon_auth_failed"
   110	    if error.status_code == 429:
   111	        return "ozon_rate_limited"
   112	    return "ozon_unavailable"
   113	
   114	
   115	async def sync_ozon_stocks(
   116	    session: AsyncSession,
   117	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   118	    seller_id: uuid.UUID,
   119	    provider: OzonMarketplaceProvider,
   120	) -> SellerStockSyncResult:
   121	    """Publish each Ozon binding's explicitly allocated pool through the provider boundary."""
   122	    bindings = list(
   123	        (
   124	            await session.execute(
   125	                select(FbsWarehouseBinding)
   126	                .where(
   127	                    FbsWarehouseBinding.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   128	                    FbsWarehouseBinding.seller_id == seller_id,
   129	                    FbsWarehouseBinding.marketplace == "ozon",
   130	                    FbsWarehouseBinding.is_active.is_(True),
   131	                    FbsWarehouseBinding.stock_sync_enabled.is_(True),
   132	                )
   133	                .order_by(FbsWarehouseBinding.external_warehouse_id)
   134	            )
   135	        )
   136	        .scalars()
   137	        .all()
   138	    )
   139	    result = SellerStockSyncResult()
   140	    if not bindings:
   141	        return result
   142	
   143	    client_id, api_key = await _credentials(session, tenant_id, seller_id)
   144	    for binding in bindings:
   145	        result.bindings_processed += 1
   146	        if binding.external_warehouse_id is None or not binding.external_warehouse_id.isdigit():
   147	            binding.last_sync_status = "error"
   148	            binding.last_error_code = "ozon_warehouse_id_invalid"
   149	            binding.last_sync_at = datetime.now(tz=UTC)
   150	            result.binding_errors += 1
   151	            result.errors += 1
   152	            continue
   153	
   154	        rows = list(
   155	            (
   156	                await session.execute(
   157	                    select(FbsBindingStockPool, ProductMarketplaceLink)
   158	                    .outerjoin(
   159	                        ProductMarketplaceLink,
   160	                        and_(
   161	                            ProductMarketplaceLink.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   162	                            ProductMarketplaceLink.seller_id == seller_id,
   163	                            ProductMarketplaceLink.product_id == FbsBindingStockPool.product_id,
   164	                            ProductMarketplaceLink.marketplace == "ozon",
   165	                            ProductMarketplaceLink.is_active.is_(True),
   166	                        ),
   167	                    )
   168	                    .where(FbsBindingStockPool.binding_id == binding.id)
   169	                    .order_by(FbsBindingStockPool.product_id)
   170	                )
   171	            ).all()
   172	        )
   173	        stocks: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   174	        missing_links = 0
   175	        for pool, link in rows:
   176	            if link is None or (not link.external_offer_id and not link.external_sku):
   177	                missing_links += 1
   178	                continue
   179	            stock: dict[str, object] = {
   180	                "warehouse_id": int(binding.external_warehouse_id),

+"""PR #140 review: physical shipment follows the packed Ozon composition."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import uuid
+from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
+
+import pytest
+import pytest_asyncio
+from sqlalchemy import select
+from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
+from sqlalchemy.orm import selectinload
+
+from app.db.session import SessionLocal, engine
+from app.models import Base
+from app.models.fbs_order import (
+    FBS_ORDER_STATUS_PACKED,
+    MAPPING_STATUS_MAPPED,
+    PACK_STATUS_PACKED,
+    RESERVE_STATUS_NO_STOCK,
+    FbsOrder,
+    FbsOrderProduct,
+)
+from app.models.fbs_packaging_fulfillment import FbsPackagingFulfillment
+from app.models.fbs_shipment_reversal_ledger import FbsShipmentReversalLedger
+from app.models.fbs_supply import (
+    FBS_DELIVERY_TYPE_WAREHOUSE_SC,
+    FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_PACKED,
+    FbsSupply,
+)
+from app.models.inventory_balance import InventoryBalance
+from app.models.inventory_movement import MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT, InventoryMovement
+from app.models.packaging_task import PackagingTask, PackagingTaskLine

+from app.models.storage_location import StorageLocation

+@pytest_asyncio.fixture
+async def db_session() -> AsyncIterator[AsyncSession]:
+    async with engine.begin() as connection:
+        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
+        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
+    async with SessionLocal() as session:
+        yield session
+    async with engine.begin() as connection:
+        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
+
