+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class _ShipmentCase:
+    tenant_id: uuid.UUID
+    supply: FbsSupply
+    order: FbsOrder
+    product_ids: tuple[uuid.UUID, ...]
+    location_ids: tuple[uuid.UUID, ...]
+    initial_quantity: int
+
+
+async def _seed_packed_ozon_order(
+    session: AsyncSession,
+    quantities: tuple[int, ...],
+) -> _ShipmentCase:
+    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
+    tenant = Tenant(name="PR140 shipment", slug=f"pr140-shipment-{suffix}")
+    seller = Seller(tenant=tenant, name="Seller")
+    warehouse = Warehouse(
+        tenant=tenant,
+        name="Ozon FBS",
+        code=f"ozon-pr140-{suffix}",
+    )
+    task = PackagingTask(tenant=tenant, warehouse=warehouse, status="done")
+    supply = FbsSupply(
+        tenant=tenant,
+        seller=seller,
+        warehouse=warehouse,
+        marketplace="ozon",
+        wb_supply_id=f"PENDING-{suffix}",
+        name="Packed Ozon supply",
+        status=FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_PACKED,
+        delivery_type=FBS_DELIVERY_TYPE_WAREHOUSE_SC,
+    )
+    now = datetime.now(UTC)
+    order = FbsOrder(
+        tenant=tenant,
+        seller=seller,
+        warehouse=warehouse,
+        supply=supply,
+        marketplace="ozon",
+        external_order_id=f"posting-{suffix}",
+        wb_order_id=-int(suffix, 16),
+        status=FBS_ORDER_STATUS_PACKED,
+        mapping_status=MAPPING_STATUS_MAPPED,
+        reserve_status=RESERVE_STATUS_NO_STOCK,
+        pack_status=PACK_STATUS_PACKED,
+        created_at_wb=now,
+        deadline_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
+    )
+    session.add_all([tenant, seller, warehouse, task, supply, order])
+    await session.flush()
+    supply.packaging_task_id = task.id
+
+    initial_quantity = 5
+    products: list[Product] = []
+    locations: list[StorageLocation] = []
+    lines: list[PackagingTaskLine] = []
+    for index, quantity in enumerate(quantities):
+        product = Product(
+            tenant=tenant,
+            seller=seller,
+            name=f"Product {index}",
+            sku_code=f"PR140-{suffix}-{index}",
+        )
+        location = StorageLocation(
+            tenant=tenant,
+            warehouse=warehouse,
+            code=f"CELL-{suffix}-{index}",
+            barcode=f"LOC-{suffix}-{index}",
+        )
+        session.add_all([product, location])
+        await session.flush()
+        line = PackagingTaskLine(
+            task=task,
+            product_id=product.id,
+            storage_location_id=location.id,
+            qty_total=quantity,
+            qty_packed_in_task=quantity,
+        )
+        position = FbsOrderProduct(
+            order=order,
+            product_id=product.id,
+            ozon_sku=10_000 + index,
+            offer_id=f"offer-{index}",
+            name=product.name,
+            quantity=quantity,
+            picked_quantity=quantity,
+            position_index=index,
+        )
+        balance = InventoryBalance(
+            tenant_id=tenant.id,
+            product_id=product.id,
+            storage_location_id=location.id,
+            quantity=initial_quantity,
+            quantity_unpacked=0,
+            quantity_packed=initial_quantity,
+        )
+        session.add_all([line, position, balance])
+        products.append(product)
+        locations.append(location)
+        lines.append(line)
+    await session.flush()
+    order.product_id = products[0].id
+
+    packed_units = [
+        {
+            "product_id": str(product.id),
+            "packaging_task_line_id": str(line.id),
+            "storage_location_id": str(location.id),
+            "idempotency_key": f"packed-{product.id}-{unit}",
+            "packed_at": now.isoformat(),
+        }
+        for product, location, line, quantity in zip(
+            products, locations, lines, quantities, strict=True
+        )
+        for unit in range(quantity)
+    ]
+    session.add(
+        FbsPackagingFulfillment(
+            tenant_id=tenant.id,
+            fbs_order_id=order.id,
+            packaging_task_id=task.id,
+            packaging_task_line_id=lines[0].id,
+            fulfilled_at=now,
+            pack_idempotency_key=f"packed-{suffix}",
+            ozon_packed_units_json=packed_units,
+        )
+    )
+    await session.commit()
+
+    loaded_order = (
+        await session.execute(
+            select(FbsOrder)
+            .where(FbsOrder.id == order.id)
+            .options(selectinload(FbsOrder.product_positions))
+        )
+    ).scalar_one()
+    loaded_supply = await session.get(FbsSupply, supply.id)
+    assert loaded_supply is not None
+    return _ShipmentCase(
+        tenant_id=tenant.id,
+        supply=loaded_supply,
+        order=loaded_order,
+        product_ids=tuple(product.id for product in products),
+        location_ids=tuple(location.id for location in locations),
+        initial_quantity=initial_quantity,
+    )
+
+
+async def _balances(session: AsyncSession, case: _ShipmentCase) -> dict[uuid.UUID, int]:
+    rows = (
+        await session.execute(
+            select(InventoryBalance).where(
+                InventoryBalance.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                InventoryBalance.product_id.in_(case.product_ids),
+            )
+        )
+    ).scalars()
+    return {row.product_id: int(row.quantity) for row in rows}
+

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_two_products_are_written_off_from_their_packed_locations(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-SHIPMENT-COMPOSITION-001
+    case = await _seed_packed_ozon_order(db_session, (1, 1))
+
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        product_id: case.initial_quantity - 1 for product_id in case.product_ids
+    }
+    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
+        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
+            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
+        )
+    )
+    assert ledger is not None
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert {
+        (position["product_id"], position["storage_location_id"], position["quantity"])
+        for position in ledger.ozon_positions_json or []
+    } == {
+        (str(product_id), str(location_id), 1)
+        for product_id, location_id in zip(
+            case.product_ids, case.location_ids, strict=True
+        )
+    }
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_quantity_above_one_is_written_off_in_full(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-SHIPMENT-COMPOSITION-002
+    case = await _seed_packed_ozon_order(db_session, (3,))
+
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 3
+    }
+    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
+        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
+            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
+        )
+    )
+    assert ledger is not None
+    assert ledger.quantity == 3
+    assert (ledger.ozon_positions_json or [])[0]["quantity"] == 3

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_cancellation_restores_every_written_off_position(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-SHIPMENT-COMPOSITION-003
+    case = await _seed_packed_ozon_order(db_session, (2, 3))
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    await _apply_status(db_session, case.order, "cancelled")
+    await db_session.flush()
+
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        product_id: case.initial_quantity for product_id in case.product_ids
+    }
+    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
+        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
+            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
+        )
+    )
+    assert ledger is not None
+    assert ledger.reversed_at is not None
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )

   643	
   644	    await session.flush()
   645	    return FbsPackProgressResult(units=units, warnings=warnings)
   646	
   647	
   648	async def _all_active_orders_fulfilled(
   649	    session: AsyncSession,
   650	    supply: FbsSupply,
   651	) -> bool:
   652	    for order in supply.orders:
   653	        if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED:
   654	            continue
   655	        if order.marketplace == "ozon":
   656	            if not await _ozon_order_pack_complete(session, order):
   657	                return False
   658	            continue
   659	        if order.product_id is None:
   660	            return False
   661	        if not await _order_has_active_fulfillment(session, order.id):
   662	            return False
   663	    return True
   664	
   665	
   666	async def _write_off_active_orders_once(
   667	    session: AsyncSession,
   668	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   669	    supply: FbsSupply,
   670	    task: PackagingTask,
   671	) -> None:
   672	    """Write off each packed order once and leave an auditable reversal unit."""
   673	    order_ids = [
   674	        order.id
   675	        for order in supply.orders
   676	        if order.product_id is not None or order.marketplace == "ozon"
   677	    ]
   678	    fulfillment_rows = (
   679	        await session.execute(
   680	            select(
   681	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.fbs_order_id,
   682	                PackagingTaskLine.product_id,
   683	                PackagingTaskLine.storage_location_id,
   684	            )
   685	            .join(
   686	                PackagingTaskLine,
   687	                PackagingTaskLine.id == FbsPackagingFulfillment.packaging_task_line_id,
   688	            )
   689	            .where(
   690	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.packaging_task_id == task.id,
   691	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.fbs_order_id.in_(order_ids),
   692	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.undone_at.is_(None),
   693	            )
   694	        )
   695	    ).all()
   696	    fulfillment_by_order: dict[uuid.UUID, tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID]] = {}
   697	    for order_id, product_id, storage_location_id in fulfillment_rows:
   698	        if order_id in fulfillment_by_order:
   699	            raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("ambiguous_fbs_packaging_fulfillment")
   700	        fulfillment_by_order[order_id] = (product_id, storage_location_id)
   701	
   702	    existing_ids = set(
   703	        (
   704	            await session.execute(
   705	                select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id).where(
   706	                    FbsShipmentReversalLedger.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   707	                    FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id.in_(order_ids),
   708	                )
   709	            )
   710	        ).scalars()
   145	        target_order.packed_at = None
   146	    return fulfillment
   147	
   148	
   149	async def write_off_order(
   150	    session: AsyncSession,
   151	    *,
   152	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   153	    order: FbsOrder,
   154	) -> None:
   155	    fulfillment = await active_order_fulfillment(session, order.id)
   156	    units = packed_units(fulfillment)
   157	    if not units or not await order_pack_complete(session, order):
   158	        raise OzonPackagingError("missing_fbs_packaging_location")
   159	    grouped: dict[tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID], int] = defaultdict(int)
   160	    for unit in units:
   161	        try:
   162	            product_id = uuid.UUID(unit["product_id"])
   163	            storage_location_id = uuid.UUID(unit["storage_location_id"])
   164	        except (KeyError, ValueError) as exc:
   165	            raise OzonPackagingError("invalid_ozon_packaging_fulfillment") from exc
   166	        grouped[(product_id, storage_location_id)] += 1
   167	    positions_json = [
   168	        {
   169	            "product_id": str(product_id),
   170	            "storage_location_id": str(storage_location_id),
   171	            "quantity": quantity,
   172	        }
   173	        for (product_id, storage_location_id), quantity in sorted(
   174	            grouped.items(), key=lambda item: (str(item[0][0]), str(item[0][1]))
   175	        )
   176	    ]
   177	    first_product_id, first_storage_location_id = next(iter(grouped))
   178	    session.add(
   179	        FbsShipmentReversalLedger(
   180	            tenant_id=tenant_id,
   181	            fbs_order_id=order.id,
   182	            product_id=first_product_id,
   183	            storage_location_id=first_storage_location_id,
   184	            quantity=sum(grouped.values()),
   185	            ozon_positions_json=positions_json,
   186	        )
   187	    )
   188	    await session.flush()
   189	    for (product_id, storage_location_id), quantity in grouped.items():
   190	        try:
   191	            await inv_svc.apply_fbs_supply_write_off(
   192	                session,
   193	                tenant_id=tenant_id,
   194	                product_id=product_id,
   195	                storage_location_id=storage_location_id,
   196	                quantity=quantity,
   197	            )
   198	        except ValueError as exc:
   199	            if str(exc) != "insufficient stock":
   200	                raise
   201	            logger.warning(
   202	                "Ozon FBS write-off skipped, no stock: tenant=%s product=%s "
   203	                "location=%s order=%s quantity=%s",
   204	                tenant_id,
   205	                product_id,
   206	                storage_location_id,
   207	                order.id,
   208	                quantity,
   209	            )

   838	
   839	async def _write_off_delivered_orders_once(
   840	    session: AsyncSession,
   841	    supply: FbsSupply,
   842	    orders: list[FbsOrder],
   843	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   844	) -> None:
   845	    """Create exactly one physical write-off per confirmed FBS order."""
   846	    active_orders = [
   847	        order
   848	        for order in orders
   849	        if order.status != FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED and order.product_id is not None
   850	    ]
   851	    order_ids = [order.id for order in active_orders]
   852	    existing_ledgers = {
   853	        ledger.fbs_order_id: ledger
   854	        for ledger in (
   855	            (
   856	                await session.execute(
   857	                    select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger)
   858	                    .where(
   859	                        FbsShipmentReversalLedger.tenant_id == supply.tenant_id,
   860	                        FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id.in_(order_ids),
   861	                    )
   862	                    .with_for_update()
   863	                )
   864	            )
   865	            .scalars()
   866	            .all()
   867	        )
   868	    }
   869	    locations = await _shipment_locations_by_order(session, order_ids)
   870	    fallback_location_id: uuid.UUID | None = None
   871	
   872	    for order in active_orders:
   873	        ledger = existing_ledgers.get(order.id)
   874	        if ledger is None:
   875	            shipment_location = locations.get(order.id)
   876	            if shipment_location is not None:
   877	                fulfilled_product_id, storage_location_id = shipment_location
   878	                if fulfilled_product_id != order.product_id:
   879	                    raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_product_mismatch", http_status=409)
   880	            else:
   881	                if fallback_location_id is None:
   882	                    fallback_location = await get_or_create_sorting_location(
   883	                        session,
   884	                        supply.tenant_id,
   885	                        supply.warehouse_id,
   886	                    )
   887	                    fallback_location_id = fallback_location.id
   888	                storage_location_id = fallback_location_id
   889	            ledger = FbsShipmentReversalLedger(
   890	                tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
   891	                fbs_order_id=order.id,
   892	                product_id=order.product_id,
   893	                storage_location_id=storage_location_id,
   894	                quantity=1,
   895	            )
   896	            session.add(ledger)
   897	            await session.flush()
   898	            existing_ledgers[order.id] = ledger
   899	
   900	        if ledger.reversed_at is not None:
   901	            raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_already_reversed", http_status=409)
   902	        if ledger.shipment_movement_id is None:
   903	            movement = await inventory_svc.apply_fbs_supply_write_off(
   904	                session,
   905	                tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
   906	                product_id=ledger.product_id,
   907	                storage_location_id=ledger.storage_location_id,
   908	                quantity=int(ledger.quantity),
   909	                actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
   910	            )
   911	            await session.flush()
   912	            ledger.shipment_movement_id = movement.id
   913	        await _release_reservation(session, order)
   914	    await session.flush()

   674	async def _all_active_orders_fulfilled(
   675	    session: AsyncSession,
   676	    supply: FbsSupply,
   677	) -> bool:
   678	    for order in supply.orders:
   679	        if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED:
   680	            continue
   681	        if order.marketplace == "ozon":
   682	            if not await _ozon_order_pack_complete(session, order):
   683	                return False
   684	            continue
   685	        if order.product_id is None:
   686	            return False
   687	        if not await _order_has_active_fulfillment(session, order.id):
   688	            return False
   689	    return True

   694	async def try_promote_fbs_supply_if_ready(
   695	    session: AsyncSession,
   696	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   697	    supply_id: uuid.UUID,
   698	    *,
   699	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   700	) -> FbsSupply | None:
   701	    supply = await _load_supply(
   702	        session,
   703	        tenant_id,
   704	        supply_id,
   705	        with_orders=True,
   706	        for_update=True,
   707	    )
   708	    if supply is None or supply.status != FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_ASSEMBLING:
   709	        return supply
   710	
   711	    task = None
   712	    if supply.packaging_task_id is not None:
   713	        task = await get_task(session, tenant_id, supply.packaging_task_id)
   714	        if task is None or not is_task_complete(task):
   715	            return supply
   716	
   717	    if not await _all_active_orders_fulfilled(session, supply):
   718	        return supply
   719	
   720	    if _supply_requires_marking(supply):
   721	        return supply
   722	
   723	    supply.status = FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_PACKED
   724	    for order in supply.orders:
   725	        if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED:
   726	            continue
   727	        if await _order_has_active_fulfillment(session, order.id):
   728	            order.status = FBS_ORDER_STATUS_PACKED
   729	    await session.flush()
   730	    return supply
   731

   771	async def _load_supply_by_packaging_task(
   772	    session: AsyncSession,
   773	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   774	    packaging_task_id: uuid.UUID,
   775	    *,
   776	    with_orders: bool = False,
   777	    for_update: bool = False,
   778	) -> FbsSupply | None:
   779	    stmt = select(FbsSupply).where(
   780	        FbsSupply.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   781	        FbsSupply.packaging_task_id == packaging_task_id,
   782	    )
   783	    if with_orders:
   784	        stmt = stmt.options(
   785	            selectinload(FbsSupply.orders).selectinload(FbsOrder.product),
   786	            selectinload(FbsSupply.orders).selectinload(FbsOrder.product_positions),
   787	            selectinload(FbsSupply.orders).selectinload(FbsOrder.markings),
   788	        )
   789	    if for_update:
   790	        stmt = stmt.with_for_update()
   791	    result = await session.execute(stmt)
   792	    return result.scalar_one_or_none()
   793	
   794	
   795	async def bind_packaging_box_to_trbx(
   796	    session: AsyncSession,
   797	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   798	    supply_id: uuid.UUID,
   799	    trbx_id: uuid.UUID,
   800	    packaging_box_id: uuid.UUID,
   801	) -> FbsTrbx:
   802	    # Binding a box to a WB cargo place (trbx) is not PVZ-specific: WB accepts
   803	    # cargo places for any delivery_type (see fbs_shipment_pvz_service module
   804	    # docstring), so a warehouse_sc supply may have trbxes to bind too.
   805	    supply = await _load_supply(
   806	        session,
   807	        tenant_id,
   808	        supply_id,
   809	        with_trbxes=True,
   810	        for_update=True,
   811	    )
   812	    if supply is None:
   813	        raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("supply_not_found")
   814	
   815	    trbx = next((row for row in supply.trbxes if row.id == trbx_id), None)
   816	    if trbx is None:
   817	        raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("trbx_not_found")
   818	
   819	    box = await session.scalar(
   820	        select(WarehouseBox)
   821	        .where(
   822	            WarehouseBox.id == packaging_box_id,
   823	            WarehouseBox.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   824	            WarehouseBox.warehouse_id == supply.warehouse_id,
   825	        )
   826	        .with_for_update()
   827	    )
   828	    if box is None:
   829	        raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("packaging_box_not_found")
   830	
   831	    already_bound = await session.scalar(
   832	        select(FbsTrbx.id).where(
   833	            FbsTrbx.packaging_box_id == packaging_box_id,
   834	            FbsTrbx.id != trbx.id,
   835	        )
   836	    )
   837	    if already_bound is not None:
   838	        raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("packaging_box_already_bound")
   839	    try:
   840	        async with session.begin_nested():
   841	            trbx.packaging_box_id = packaging_box_id
   842	            await session.flush()
   843	    except IntegrityError as exc:
   844	        raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("packaging_box_already_bound") from exc
   845	    return trbx

    fix(fbs): убрать двойное списание остатка при упаковке
    
    Эталон перенёс списание остатка ФБС с упаковки на подтверждённую доставку.
    При сведении веток я сохранил старый вызов на упаковке, и списание стало
    происходить дважды: на упаковке и на доставке. Второй раз остатка уже не
    хватает — поставка встаёт с «insufficient stock», а до этого товар списан вдвое.
    
    Списание теперь только там, где его оставил эталон. Мёртвая функция удалена.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

   321	async def _physical_on_hand_in_warehouse(
   322	    session: AsyncSession,
   323	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   324	    warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
   325	    product_id: uuid.UUID,
   326	) -> int:
   327	    stmt = (
   328	        select(func.coalesce(func.sum(InventoryBalance.quantity), 0))
   329	        .join(StorageLocation, StorageLocation.id == InventoryBalance.storage_location_id)
   330	        .where(
   331	            InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   332	            InventoryBalance.product_id == product_id,
   333	            StorageLocation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   334	            StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
   335	        )
   336	    )
   337	    return int(await session.scalar(stmt) or 0)
   338	
   339	
   340	async def storage_on_hand_in_warehouse(
   341	    session: AsyncSession,
   342	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   343	    warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
   344	    product_id: uuid.UUID,
   345	) -> int:
   346	    """Остаток в ячейках хранения (без зоны «Сортировка»)."""
   347	    stmt = (
   348	        select(func.coalesce(func.sum(InventoryBalance.quantity), 0))
   349	        .join(StorageLocation, StorageLocation.id == InventoryBalance.storage_location_id)
   350	        .where(
   351	            InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   352	            InventoryBalance.product_id == product_id,
   353	            StorageLocation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   354	            StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
   355	            StorageLocation.code != SORTING_LOCATION_CODE,
   356	        )
   357	    )
   358	    return int(await session.scalar(stmt) or 0)

   361	async def sorting_on_hand_in_warehouse(
   362	    session: AsyncSession,
   363	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   364	    warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
   365	    product_id: uuid.UUID,
   366	) -> int:
   367	    """Остаток в зоне «Сортировка» (буфер до раскладки)."""
   368	    stmt = (
   369	        select(func.coalesce(func.sum(InventoryBalance.quantity), 0))
   370	        .join(StorageLocation, StorageLocation.id == InventoryBalance.storage_location_id)
   371	        .where(
   372	            InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   373	            InventoryBalance.product_id == product_id,
   374	            StorageLocation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   375	            StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
   376	            StorageLocation.code == SORTING_LOCATION_CODE,
   377	        )
   378	    )
   379	    return int(await session.scalar(stmt) or 0)

   384	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   385	    product_id: uuid.UUID,
   386	) -> None:

   393	async def sync_outbound_line_reservation(
   394	    session: AsyncSession,
   395	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   396	    request: OutboundShipmentRequest,
   397	    line: OutboundShipmentLine,
   398	) -> None:
   399	    await session.execute(
   400	        delete(InventoryReservation).where(
   401	            InventoryReservation.outbound_shipment_line_id == line.id,
   402	        )
   403	    )
   404	
   405	    # Резерв под исходящую отгрузку вычитается из доступного для ФБС
   406	    # (`fbs_available_qty_by_product` минусует `_outbound_reserved_by_product`).
   407	    # Значит и постановка, и снятие резерва меняют цифру, которую должен видеть WB.
   408	    # Ставим публикацию здесь, до всех ранних `return`, чтобы не потерять ни один путь.
   409	    await _schedule_fbs_publish_for_product(session, tenant_id, line.product_id)
   410	
   411	    should_hold = request.status in OUTBOUND_RESERVE_STATUSES and line.shipped_qty < line.quantity
   412	    if not should_hold:
   413	        return
   414	
   415	    desired = line.quantity - line.shipped_qty
   416	    if desired < 1:
   417	        return
   418	
   419	    sid = line.storage_location_id
   420	    if sid is not None:
   421	        on_hand = await _physical_on_hand(session, tenant_id, line.product_id, sid)
   422	        others = await reserved_qty_excluding_line(
   423	            session, tenant_id, line.product_id, sid, line.id
   424	        )
   425	        if on_hand < others + desired:
   426	            raise ValueError(RESERVATION_ERROR)
   427	        session.add(
   428	            InventoryReservation(
   429	                tenant_id=tenant_id,
   430	                outbound_shipment_line_id=line.id,
   431	                product_id=line.product_id,
   432	                storage_location_id=sid,
   433	                warehouse_id=None,
   434	                quantity=desired,
   435	            )
   436	        )
   437	        return
   438	
   439	    wh_id = request.warehouse_id
   440	    on_hand_wh = await storage_on_hand_in_warehouse(session, tenant_id, wh_id, line.product_id)
   441	    rsv_map = await reserved_totals_by_product(
   442	        session,
   443	        tenant_id,
   444	        [line.product_id],
   445	        warehouse_id=wh_id,
   446	    )
   447	    already = int(rsv_map.get(line.product_id, 0))
   448	    if on_hand_wh < already + desired:
   449	        raise ValueError(RESERVATION_ERROR)
   450	
   451	    session.add(
   452	        InventoryReservation(
   453	            tenant_id=tenant_id,
   454	            outbound_shipment_line_id=line.id,
   455	            product_id=line.product_id,
   456	            storage_location_id=None,
   457	            warehouse_id=wh_id,
   458	            quantity=desired,
   459	        )
   460	    )
   461	
   462	
   463	async def list_balances_at_location(
   464	    session: AsyncSession,
   465	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   466	    storage_location_id: uuid.UUID,
   467	    *,
   468	    seller_product_owner_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
   469	) -> list[tuple[InventoryBalance, Product, int]] | None:
   470	    loc = await session.get(StorageLocation, storage_location_id)
   471	    if loc is None or loc.tenant_id != tenant_id:
   472	        return None
   473	    stmt = (
   474	        select(InventoryBalance, Product)
   475	        .join(Product, Product.id == InventoryBalance.product_id)
   476	        .where(
   477	            InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   478	            InventoryBalance.storage_location_id == storage_location_id,
   479	        )
   480	        .options(selectinload(Product.seller))
   481	        .order_by(Product.sku_code)
   482	    )
   483	    if seller_product_owner_id is not None:
   484	        stmt = stmt.where(Product.seller_id == seller_product_owner_id)
   485	    res = await session.execute(stmt)
   486	    pairs = [(b, p) for b, p in res.all()]
   487	    if not pairs:
   488	        return []
   489	    pids = list({b.product_id for b, _ in pairs})
   490	    rsv_map = await reserved_totals_by_product_at_location(
   491	        session, tenant_id, storage_location_id, pids
   492	    )
   493	    return [(b, p, int(rsv_map.get(b.product_id, 0))) for b, p in pairs]
   494

   496	async def record_movement_and_adjust_balance(
   497	    session: AsyncSession,
   498	    *,
   499	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   500	    product_id: uuid.UUID,
   501	    storage_location_id: uuid.UUID,
   502	    quantity_delta: int,
   503	    movement_type: str,
   504	    inbound_intake_line_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
   505	    outbound_shipment_line_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
   506	    inventory_count_line_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
   507	    transfer_group_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
   508	    marketplace_unload_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
   509	    # Разрешить остатку уйти в минус. По умолчанию НЕЛЬЗЯ: минус на полке —
   510	    # бессмыслица, и защита от него включена для всех путей. Исключение ровно
   511	    # одно — подтверждённая доставка FBS: маркетплейс сказал, что товар уехал,
   512	    # значит его на складе нет независимо от того, что думает учёт. Отказать
   513	    # там значит навсегда подвесить поставку и оставить призрачный остаток.
   514	    # Минус в этом случае — честная запись расхождения, а не поломка.
   515	    allow_negative: bool = False,
   516	    # Автор движения обязателен: движение без человека в журнале не расследуется.
   517	    # Аргументы после «*» именованные, поэтому обязательный может стоять
   518	    # за теми, у кого есть значение по умолчанию.
   519	    actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
   520	    deduct_prefer: DeductPrefer = "unpacked",
     1	from __future__ import annotations

    28	class InventoryBalance(Base):
    29	    """Остаток SKU в ячейке. Меняется только проведёнными операциями."""
    30	
    31	    __tablename__ = "inventory_balances"
    32	    __table_args__ = (
    33	        CheckConstraint(
    34	            "(container_kind IS NULL AND container_id IS NULL) OR "
    35	            "(container_kind IS NOT NULL AND container_id IS NOT NULL)",
    36	            name="ck_inventory_balance_container_pair",
    37	        ),
    38	        CheckConstraint(
    39	            "container_kind IS NULL OR container_kind IN ('pallet', 'box', 'cargo_place')",
    40	            name="ck_inventory_balance_container_kind",
    41	        ),
    42	        # Запрета на отрицательный остаток здесь НЕТ, и это решение владельца.
    43	        #
    44	        # Минус на полке бессмыслен, и защита от него нужна — но проверка в базе
    45	        # не умеет спрашивать, кто её вызвал, а ровно одному пути минус нужен:
    46	        # подтверждённой доставке FBS. Маркетплейс сказал, что товар уехал —
    47	        # значит на складе его нет, что бы ни думал учёт. Отказать значит
    48	        # подвесить поставку навсегда и оставить призрачный остаток.
    49	        #
    50	        # Поэтому защита переехала в сервис: `record_movement_and_adjust_balance`
    51	        # по умолчанию в минус не пускает, и делает это условием внутри самого
    52	        # запроса — то есть остаётся устойчивой к одновременным списаниям.
    53	        # Обойти её можно только явным `allow_negative=True`, и такой вызов в
    54	        # коде один.
    55	        Index(
    56	            "uq_inventory_balance_loc_product_container",
    57	            "storage_location_id",
    58	            "product_id",
    59	            text("coalesce(container_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')"),
    60	            unique=True,
    61	        ),
    62	    )
    63	
    64	    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
    65	        Uuid(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4
    66	    )
    67	    tenant_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
    68	        Uuid(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("tenants.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True
    69	    )
    70	    storage_location_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
    71	        Uuid(as_uuid=True),
    72	        ForeignKey("storage_locations.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
    73	        index=True,
    74	    )
    75	    product_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
    76	        Uuid(as_uuid=True),
    77	        ForeignKey("products.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
    78	        index=True,
    79	    )
    80	    container_kind: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
    81	        String(32), nullable=True, index=True
    82	    )
    83	    container_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(
    84	        Uuid(as_uuid=True), nullable=True, index=True
    85	    )
    86	    quantity: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    87	    quantity_unpacked: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    88	    quantity_packed: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    89	    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
    90	        DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), nullable=False
    91	    )
    92	
    93	    tenant: Mapped[Tenant] = relationship("Tenant", back_populates="inventory_balances")
    94	    storage_location: Mapped[StorageLocation] = relationship(
    95	        "StorageLocation", back_populates="inventory_balances"
    96	    )
    97	    product: Mapped[Product] = relationship(
    98	        "Product", back_populates="inventory_balances"
    99	    )

   665	
   666	async def _write_off_active_orders_once(
   667	    session: AsyncSession,
   668	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   669	    supply: FbsSupply,
   670	    task: PackagingTask,
   671	) -> None:
   672	    """Write off each packed order once and leave an auditable reversal unit."""
   673	    order_ids = [
   674	        order.id
   675	        for order in supply.orders
   676	        if order.product_id is not None or order.marketplace == "ozon"
   677	    ]
   678	    fulfillment_rows = (
   679	        await session.execute(
   680	            select(
   681	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.fbs_order_id,
   682	                PackagingTaskLine.product_id,
   683	                PackagingTaskLine.storage_location_id,
   684	            )
   685	            .join(
   686	                PackagingTaskLine,
   687	                PackagingTaskLine.id == FbsPackagingFulfillment.packaging_task_line_id,
   688	            )
   689	            .where(
   690	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.packaging_task_id == task.id,
   691	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.fbs_order_id.in_(order_ids),
   692	                FbsPackagingFulfillment.undone_at.is_(None),
   693	            )
   694	        )
   695	    ).all()
   696	    fulfillment_by_order: dict[uuid.UUID, tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID]] = {}
   697	    for order_id, product_id, storage_location_id in fulfillment_rows:
   698	        if order_id in fulfillment_by_order:
   699	            raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("ambiguous_fbs_packaging_fulfillment")
   700	        fulfillment_by_order[order_id] = (product_id, storage_location_id)
   701	
   702	    existing_ids = set(
   703	        (
   704	            await session.execute(
   705	                select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id).where(
   706	                    FbsShipmentReversalLedger.tenant_id == tenant_id,
   707	                    FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id.in_(order_ids),
   708	                )
   709	            )
   710	        ).scalars()
   711	    )
   712	    for order in supply.orders:
   713	        if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED:
   714	            continue
   715	        if order.id in existing_ids:
   716	            continue
   717	        if order.marketplace == "ozon":
   718	            try:
   719	                await _write_off_ozon_order(session, tenant_id=tenant_id, order=order)
   720	            except OzonPackagingError as exc:
   721	                raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError(str(exc)) from exc
   722	            continue
   723	        if order.product_id is None:
   724	            continue
   725	        fulfillment = fulfillment_by_order.get(order.id)
   726	        if fulfillment is None:
   727	            raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("missing_fbs_packaging_location")
   728	        fulfilled_product_id, storage_location_id = fulfillment
   729	        if fulfilled_product_id != order.product_id:
   730	            raise FbsPackagingIntegrationError("fbs_packaging_product_mismatch")
   731	        session.add(
   732	            FbsShipmentReversalLedger(
   733	                tenant_id=tenant_id,
   734	                fbs_order_id=order.id,
   735	                product_id=order.product_id,
   736	                storage_location_id=storage_location_id,
   737	                quantity=1,
   738	            )
   739	        )
   740	        await session.flush()
   741	        # Тот же принцип, что и на самой упаковке: нехватка остатка в ячейке не должна
   742	        # останавливать поставку. Иначе заказ, упакованный без списания (товар лежал в
   743	        # сортировке другого склада), упирался бы в отказ на завершении упаковки — то
   744	        # есть склад вставал бы на шаг позже, чем раньше.
   745	        try:
   746	            await inv_svc.apply_fbs_supply_write_off(
   747	                session,
   748	                tenant_id=tenant_id,
   749	                product_id=order.product_id,
   750	                storage_location_id=storage_location_id,
   751	                quantity=1,
   752	            )
   753	        except ValueError as exc:
   754	            if str(exc) != "insufficient stock":
   755	                raise
   756	            logger.warning(
   757	                "fbs write-off skipped, no stock: tenant=%s product=%s location=%s order=%s",
   758	                tenant_id,
   759	                order.product_id,
   760	                storage_location_id,
   761	                order.id,
   762	            )
   763	
   764	
   765	async def try_promote_fbs_supply_if_ready(
   766	    session: AsyncSession,
   767	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   768	    supply_id: uuid.UUID,
   769	) -> FbsSupply | None:
   770	    supply = await _load_supply(
   771	        session,
   772	        tenant_id,
   773	        supply_id,
   774	        with_orders=True,
   775	        for_update=True,
   776	    )
   777	    if supply is None or supply.status != FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_ASSEMBLING:
   778	        return supply
   779	
   780	    task = None
   781	    if supply.packaging_task_id is not None:
   782	        task = await get_task(session, tenant_id, supply.packaging_task_id)
   783	        if task is None or not is_task_complete(task):
   784	            return supply
   785	
   786	    if not await _all_active_orders_fulfilled(session, supply):
   787	        return supply
   788	
   789	    if _supply_requires_marking(supply):
   790	        return supply
   791	
   792	    supply.status = FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_PACKED
   793	    for order in supply.orders:
   794	        if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED:
   795	            continue
   796	        if await _order_has_active_fulfillment(session, order.id):
   797	            order.status = FBS_ORDER_STATUS_PACKED
   798	    await session.flush()
   799	    return supply
   800	
   801	
   802	async def sync_fbs_supply_on_packaging_done(
   803	    session: AsyncSession,
   804	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   805	    packaging_task_id: uuid.UUID,
   806	) -> FbsSupply | None:
   807	    supply = await _load_supply_by_packaging_task(
   808	        session, tenant_id, packaging_task_id, with_orders=True
   809	    )

"""PR #140 review: physical shipment follows the packed Ozon composition."""

@pytest_asyncio.fixture
async def db_session() -> AsyncIterator[AsyncSession]:
    async with engine.begin() as connection:
        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        yield session
    async with engine.begin() as connection:
        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _ShipmentCase:
    tenant_id: uuid.UUID
    supply: FbsSupply
    order: FbsOrder
    product_ids: tuple[uuid.UUID, ...]
    location_ids: tuple[uuid.UUID, ...]
    initial_quantity: int


async def _seed_packed_ozon_order(
    session: AsyncSession,
    quantities: tuple[int, ...],
) -> _ShipmentCase:
    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
    tenant = Tenant(name="PR140 shipment", slug=f"pr140-shipment-{suffix}")
    seller = Seller(tenant=tenant, name="Seller")
    warehouse = Warehouse(
        tenant=tenant,
        name="Ozon FBS",
        code=f"ozon-pr140-{suffix}",
    )
    task = PackagingTask(tenant=tenant, warehouse=warehouse, status="done")
    supply = FbsSupply(
        tenant=tenant,
        seller=seller,
        warehouse=warehouse,
        marketplace="ozon",
        wb_supply_id=f"PENDING-{suffix}",
        name="Packed Ozon supply",
        status=FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_PACKED,
        delivery_type=FBS_DELIVERY_TYPE_WAREHOUSE_SC,
    )
    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    order = FbsOrder(
        tenant=tenant,
        seller=seller,
        warehouse=warehouse,
        supply=supply,
        marketplace="ozon",
        external_order_id=f"posting-{suffix}",
        wb_order_id=-int(suffix, 16),
        status=FBS_ORDER_STATUS_PACKED,
        mapping_status=MAPPING_STATUS_MAPPED,
        reserve_status=RESERVE_STATUS_NO_STOCK,
        pack_status=PACK_STATUS_PACKED,
        created_at_wb=now,
        deadline_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
    )
    session.add_all([tenant, seller, warehouse, task, supply, order])
    await session.flush()
    supply.packaging_task_id = task.id

    initial_quantity = 5
    products: list[Product] = []
    locations: list[StorageLocation] = []
    lines: list[PackagingTaskLine] = []
    for index, quantity in enumerate(quantities):
        product = Product(
            tenant=tenant,
            seller=seller,
            name=f"Product {index}",
            sku_code=f"PR140-{suffix}-{index}",
        )
        location = StorageLocation(
        location_ids=tuple(location.id for location in locations),
        initial_quantity=initial_quantity,
    )


async def _balances(session: AsyncSession, case: _ShipmentCase) -> dict[uuid.UUID, int]:
    rows = (
        await session.execute(
            select(InventoryBalance).where(
                InventoryBalance.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
                InventoryBalance.product_id.in_(case.product_ids),
            )
        )
    ).scalars()
    return {row.product_id: int(row.quantity) for row in rows}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ozon_two_products_are_written_off_from_their_packed_locations(
    db_session: AsyncSession,
) -> None:
    # TC-NEW-FBS-SHIPMENT-COMPOSITION-001
    case = await _seed_packed_ozon_order(db_session, (1, 1))

    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)

    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
        product_id: case.initial_quantity - 1 for product_id in case.product_ids
    }
    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
        )
    )
    assert ledger is not None
    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
    assert {
        (position["product_id"], position["storage_location_id"], position["quantity"])
        for position in ledger.ozon_positions_json or []
    } == {
        (str(product_id), str(location_id), 1)
        for product_id, location_id in zip(
            case.product_ids, case.location_ids, strict=True
        )
    }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ozon_quantity_above_one_is_written_off_in_full(
    db_session: AsyncSession,
) -> None:
    # TC-NEW-FBS-SHIPMENT-COMPOSITION-002
    case = await _seed_packed_ozon_order(db_session, (3,))

    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)

    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 3
    }
    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
        )
    )
    assert ledger is not None
    assert ledger.quantity == 3
    assert (ledger.ozon_positions_json or [])[0]["quantity"] == 3


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ozon_cancellation_restores_every_written_off_position(
    db_session: AsyncSession,
) -> None:
    # TC-NEW-FBS-SHIPMENT-COMPOSITION-003
    case = await _seed_packed_ozon_order(db_session, (2, 3))
    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)

    await _apply_status(db_session, case.order, "cancelled")
    await db_session.flush()

    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
        product_id: case.initial_quantity for product_id in case.product_ids
    }
    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
        )
    )
    assert ledger is not None
    assert ledger.reversed_at is not None
    movements = list(
        (
            await db_session.execute(
                select(InventoryMovement).where(
                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
                )
            )
        )
        .scalars()
        .all()
    )
    assert sorted(int(movement.quantity_delta) for movement in movements) == [-3, -2, 2, 3]

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_packaging_write_off_then_delivery_debits_stock_once(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-001: старый упаковочный вызов оставляет
+    # доставке заполненную ссылку на движение, поэтому повторного расхода нет.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+
+    ledger = await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_wb_delivery_keeps_single_write_off_path(db_session: AsyncSession) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-002: защита Ozon не меняет прежний путь WB.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,), marketplace="wb")
+
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
+        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
+            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
+        )
+    )
+    assert ledger is not None
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_cancelled_after_packaging_is_not_written_off_again(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-003: отмена сторнирует старое упаковочное
+    # списание, а доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход отменённого заказа.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+    await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+
+    await _apply_status(db_session, case.order, "cancelled")
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert sorted(int(movement.quantity_delta) for movement in movements) == [-1, 1]

+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_wb_delivery_keeps_single_write_off_path(db_session: AsyncSession) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-002: защита Ozon не меняет прежний путь WB.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,), marketplace="wb")
+
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
+        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
+            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
+        )
+    )
+    assert ledger is not None
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_cancelled_after_packaging_is_not_written_off_again(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-003: отмена сторнирует старое упаковочное
+    # списание, а доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход отменённого заказа.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+    await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+
+    await _apply_status(db_session, case.order, "cancelled")
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert sorted(int(movement.quantity_delta) for movement in movements) == [-1, 1]

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_packaging_write_off_then_delivery_debits_stock_once(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-001: старый упаковочный вызов оставляет
+    # доставке заполненную ссылку на движение, поэтому повторного расхода нет.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+
+    ledger = await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_wb_delivery_keeps_single_write_off_path(db_session: AsyncSession) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-002: защита Ozon не меняет прежний путь WB.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,), marketplace="wb")
+
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
+        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
+            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
+        )
+    )
+    assert ledger is not None
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_cancelled_after_packaging_is_not_written_off_again(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-003: отмена сторнирует старое упаковочное
+    # списание, а доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход отменённого заказа.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+    await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+
+    await _apply_status(db_session, case.order, "cancelled")
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert sorted(int(movement.quantity_delta) for movement in movements) == [-1, 1]

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_packaging_write_off_then_delivery_debits_stock_once(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-001: старый упаковочный вызов оставляет
+    # доставке заполненную ссылку на движение, поэтому повторного расхода нет.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+
+    ledger = await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]

@@ -0,0 +1,39 @@
+# Итог: двойное списание Ozon
+
+## Решение
+
+Оставлена единая точка фактического списания остатка FBS — подтверждённая
+доставка. Завершение упаковки переводит количество между состояниями
+`quantity_unpacked` и `quantity_packed`, но не уменьшает общий остаток
+`quantity`. Поэтому Ozon и WB проходят через один доставочный барьер, а рабочий
+путь WB не меняется.
+
+Дополнительно сохранена защита для старого Ozon-пути: функция
+`fbs_ozon_packaging_service.write_off_order` создаёт
+`FbsShipmentReversalLedger`, выполняет расход и записывает идентификатор первого
+движения в `shipment_movement_id`. Доставочный сервис читает ту же таблицу и при
+заполненном `shipment_movement_id` повторный расход не создаёт. Исключать все
+заказы Ozon из доставочного списания нельзя: в текущем процессе именно доставка
+является единственной штатной точкой уменьшения общего остатка.
+
+В исходном состоянии этой ветки обе части исправления уже присутствовали:
+списание при завершении упаковки удалено коммитом `2178e3ea`, а заполнение
+`shipment_movement_id` добавлено коммитом `250fb180`. В рамках этой задачи
+добавлены недостающие прямые регрессии, которые воспроизводят опасную
+последовательность и не дают защите исчезнуть незаметно.
+
+## Проверки тестами
+
+В `backend/tests/test_fbs_pr140_shipment_write_off.py` закреплены три сценария:
+
+1. Упакованный Ozon-заказ уже имеет расход и расписку, затем проходит доставку:
+   остаток уменьшается один раз, а в журнале остаётся одно движение `-1`,
+   связанное с `shipment_movement_id`.
+2. WB-заказ дважды попадает в идемпотентный доставочный обработчик: прежний путь
+   создаёт ровно одно движение `-1` и уменьшает остаток один раз.
+3. Ozon-заказ отменён после упаковочного расхода: расход сторнируется движением
+   `+1`, а последующий доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход.
+
+## Ворота
+
+Результаты обязательных команд будут записаны после итогового прогона.

+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-002: защита Ozon не меняет прежний путь WB.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,), marketplace="wb")
+
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
+        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
+            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
+        )
+    )
+    assert ledger is not None
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_cancelled_after_packaging_is_not_written_off_again(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-003: отмена сторнирует старое упаковочное
+    # списание, а доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход отменённого заказа.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+    await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+
+    await _apply_status(db_session, case.order, "cancelled")
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert sorted(int(movement.quantity_delta) for movement in movements) == [-1, 1]

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..bf9d2a6ad9fa2b7379a0bc568fe5015d5e5c43fa

+++ b/tasks/_zhurnal/codex-briefs/ITOG-ozon-dvoynoe-spisanie.md
@@ -0,0 +1,39 @@
+# Итог: двойное списание Ozon
+
+## Решение
+
+Оставлена единая точка фактического списания остатка FBS — подтверждённая
+доставка. Завершение упаковки переводит количество между состояниями
+`quantity_unpacked` и `quantity_packed`, но не уменьшает общий остаток
+`quantity`. Поэтому Ozon и WB проходят через один доставочный барьер, а рабочий
+путь WB не меняется.
+
+Дополнительно сохранена защита для старого Ozon-пути: функция
+`fbs_ozon_packaging_service.write_off_order` создаёт
+`FbsShipmentReversalLedger`, выполняет расход и записывает идентификатор первого
+движения в `shipment_movement_id`. Доставочный сервис читает ту же таблицу и при
+заполненном `shipment_movement_id` повторный расход не создаёт. Исключать все
+заказы Ozon из доставочного списания нельзя: в текущем процессе именно доставка
+является единственной штатной точкой уменьшения общего остатка.
+
+В исходном состоянии этой ветки обе части исправления уже присутствовали:
+списание при завершении упаковки удалено коммитом `2178e3ea`, а заполнение
+`shipment_movement_id` добавлено коммитом `250fb180`. В рамках этой задачи
+добавлены недостающие прямые регрессии, которые воспроизводят опасную
+последовательность и не дают защите исчезнуть незаметно.
+
+## Проверки тестами
+
+В `backend/tests/test_fbs_pr140_shipment_write_off.py` закреплены три сценария:
+
+1. Упакованный Ozon-заказ уже имеет расход и расписку, затем проходит доставку:
+   остаток уменьшается один раз, а в журнале остаётся одно движение `-1`,
+   связанное с `shipment_movement_id`.
+2. WB-заказ дважды попадает в идемпотентный доставочный обработчик: прежний путь
+   создаёт ровно одно движение `-1` и уменьшает остаток один раз.
+3. Ozon-заказ отменён после упаковочного расхода: расход сторнируется движением
+   `+1`, а последующий доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход.
+
+## Ворота
+
+Результаты обязательных команд будут записаны после итогового прогона.

     )
     assert ledger is not None
     assert ledger.quantity == 1

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_packaging_write_off_then_delivery_debits_stock_once(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-001: старый упаковочный вызов оставляет
+    # доставке заполненную ссылку на движение, поэтому повторного расхода нет.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+
+    ledger = await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_wb_delivery_keeps_single_write_off_path(db_session: AsyncSession) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-002: защита Ozon не меняет прежний путь WB.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,), marketplace="wb")
+
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
+        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
+            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
+        )
+    )
+    assert ledger is not None
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_cancelled_after_packaging_is_not_written_off_again(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-003: отмена сторнирует старое упаковочное
+    # списание, а доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход отменённого заказа.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+    await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+
+    await _apply_status(db_session, case.order, "cancelled")
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert sorted(int(movement.quantity_delta) for movement in movements) == [-1, 1]

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..bf9d2a6ad9fa2b7379a0bc568fe5015d5e5c43fa

@@ -0,0 +1,39 @@
+# Итог: двойное списание Ozon
+
+## Решение
+
+Оставлена единая точка фактического списания остатка FBS — подтверждённая
+доставка. Завершение упаковки переводит количество между состояниями
+`quantity_unpacked` и `quantity_packed`, но не уменьшает общий остаток
+`quantity`. Поэтому Ozon и WB проходят через один доставочный барьер, а рабочий
+путь WB не меняется.
+
+Дополнительно сохранена защита для старого Ozon-пути: функция
+`fbs_ozon_packaging_service.write_off_order` создаёт
+`FbsShipmentReversalLedger`, выполняет расход и записывает идентификатор первого
+движения в `shipment_movement_id`. Доставочный сервис читает ту же таблицу и при
+заполненном `shipment_movement_id` повторный расход не создаёт. Исключать все
+заказы Ozon из доставочного списания нельзя: в текущем процессе именно доставка
+является единственной штатной точкой уменьшения общего остатка.
+
+В исходном состоянии этой ветки обе части исправления уже присутствовали:
+списание при завершении упаковки удалено коммитом `2178e3ea`, а заполнение
+`shipment_movement_id` добавлено коммитом `250fb180`. В рамках этой задачи
+добавлены недостающие прямые регрессии, которые воспроизводят опасную
+последовательность и не дают защите исчезнуть незаметно.
+
+## Проверки тестами
+
+В `backend/tests/test_fbs_pr140_shipment_write_off.py` закреплены три сценария:
+
+1. Упакованный Ozon-заказ уже имеет расход и расписку, затем проходит доставку:
+   остаток уменьшается один раз, а в журнале остаётся одно движение `-1`,
+   связанное с `shipment_movement_id`.
+2. WB-заказ дважды попадает в идемпотентный доставочный обработчик: прежний путь
+   создаёт ровно одно движение `-1` и уменьшает остаток один раз.
+3. Ozon-заказ отменён после упаковочного расхода: расход сторнируется движением
+   `+1`, а последующий доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход.
+
+## Ворота
+
+Результаты обязательных команд будут записаны после итогового прогона.

+async def test_ozon_packaging_write_off_then_delivery_debits_stock_once(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-001: старый упаковочный вызов оставляет
+    # доставке заполненную ссылку на движение, поэтому повторного расхода нет.

+
+    ledger = await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+

+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_wb_delivery_keeps_single_write_off_path(db_session: AsyncSession) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-002: защита Ozon не меняет прежний путь WB.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,), marketplace="wb")
+
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    ledger = await db_session.scalar(
+        select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
+            FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id == case.order.id
+        )
+    )
+    assert ledger is not None
+    assert ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity - 1
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert [(movement.id, int(movement.quantity_delta)) for movement in movements] == [
+        (ledger.shipment_movement_id, -1)
+    ]

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_ozon_cancelled_after_packaging_is_not_written_off_again(
+    db_session: AsyncSession,
+) -> None:
+    # TC-NEW-FBS-OZON-WRITE-OFF-003: отмена сторнирует старое упаковочное
+    # списание, а доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход отменённого заказа.
+    case = await _seed_packed_order(db_session, (1,))
+    await write_off_ozon_order(
+        db_session,
+        tenant_id=case.tenant_id,
+        order=case.order,
+        actor_user_id=None,
+    )
+
+    await _apply_status(db_session, case.order, "cancelled")
+    await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(db_session, case.supply, [case.order], None)
+
+    assert await _balances(db_session, case) == {
+        case.product_ids[0]: case.initial_quantity
+    }
+    movements = list(
+        (
+            await db_session.execute(
+                select(InventoryMovement).where(
+                    InventoryMovement.tenant_id == case.tenant_id,
+                    InventoryMovement.product_id == case.product_ids[0],
+                    InventoryMovement.movement_type == MOVEMENT_TYPE_FBS_SHIPMENT,
+                )
+            )
+        )
+        .scalars()
+        .all()
+    )
+    assert sorted(int(movement.quantity_delta) for movement in movements) == [-1, 1]

+# Итог: двойное списание Ozon
+
+## Решение
+
+Оставлена единая точка фактического списания остатка FBS — подтверждённая
+доставка. Завершение упаковки переводит количество между состояниями
+`quantity_unpacked` и `quantity_packed`, но не уменьшает общий остаток
+`quantity`. Поэтому Ozon и WB проходят через один доставочный барьер, а рабочий
+путь WB не меняется.
+
+Дополнительно сохранена защита для старого Ozon-пути: функция
+`fbs_ozon_packaging_service.write_off_order` создаёт
+`FbsShipmentReversalLedger`, выполняет расход и записывает идентификатор первого
+движения в `shipment_movement_id`. Доставочный сервис читает ту же таблицу и при
+заполненном `shipment_movement_id` повторный расход не создаёт. Исключать все
+заказы Ozon из доставочного списания нельзя: в текущем процессе именно доставка
+является единственной штатной точкой уменьшения общего остатка.
+
+В исходном состоянии этой ветки обе части исправления уже присутствовали:
+списание при завершении упаковки удалено коммитом `2178e3ea`, а заполнение
+`shipment_movement_id` добавлено коммитом `250fb180`. В рамках этой задачи
+добавлены недостающие прямые регрессии, которые воспроизводят опасную
+последовательность и не дают защите исчезнуть незаметно.
+
+## Проверки тестами
+
+В `backend/tests/test_fbs_pr140_shipment_write_off.py` закреплены три сценария:
+
+1. Упакованный Ozon-заказ уже имеет расход и расписку, затем проходит доставку:
+   остаток уменьшается один раз, а в журнале остаётся одно движение `-1`,
+   связанное с `shipment_movement_id`.
+2. WB-заказ дважды попадает в идемпотентный доставочный обработчик: прежний путь
+   создаёт ровно одно движение `-1` и уменьшает остаток один раз.
+3. Ozon-заказ отменён после упаковочного расхода: расход сторнируется движением
+   `+1`, а последующий доставочный обработчик не создаёт новый расход.
+
+## Ворота
+
+Результаты обязательных команд будут записаны после итогового прогона.
